debug_success = {
	add_treasury = 50000
}
debug_fail = {
	add_treasury = -50000
}

assert = {
	assert_if = {
		limit = { always = yes }
	}
}


set_all_colony = {
	every_state_in_africa = {
		set_state_owner = c:GBR
		set_state_type = unincorporated
	}

}
﻿partition_ottoman_balkans = {
    # release countries if they dont exist
    if = {
        limit = {
            NOT = {
                exists = c:ALB
            }
            any_scope_state = {
                region = sr:region_balkans
                state_region = {
                    is_homeland = cu:albanian
                }
            }
        }
        random_scope_state = {
            limit = {
                region = sr:region_balkans
                state_region = {
                    is_homeland = cu:albanian
                }
            }
            save_scope_as = albania_scope
        }
        create_country = {
            tag = ALB
            origin = root

            state = scope:albania_scope
        }
        c:ALB ?= {
            if = {
                    limit = {
                        sr:region_balkans = {
                            any_scope_state = {
                            state_region = {
                            is_homeland = cu:albanian
                            }
                            owner = {
                                NOT = { this = root }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    NOT = {
                        has_journal_entry = je_spoils_of_war
                    }
                }
                add_journal_entry = {
                    type = je_spoils_of_war
                }
            }
        }
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            NOT = {
                exists = c:BUL
            }
            any_scope_state = {
                region = sr:region_balkans
                state_region = {
                    is_homeland = cu:bulgarian
                }
            }
        }
        random_scope_state = {
            limit = {
                region = sr:region_balkans
                state_region = {
                    is_homeland = cu:bulgarian
                }
            }
            save_scope_as = bulgaria_scope
        }
        create_country = {
            tag = BUL
            origin = root

            state = scope:bulgaria_scope
        }
        c:BUL ?= {
            if = {
                limit = {
                    sr:region_balkans = {
                        any_scope_state = {
                            state_region = {
                                is_homeland = cu:bulgarian
                            }
                            owner = {
                                NOT = { this = root }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    NOT = {
                        has_journal_entry = je_spoils_of_war
                    }
                }
                add_journal_entry = {
                    type = je_spoils_of_war
                }
            }
        }
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            NOT = {
                exists = c:BOS
            }
            any_scope_state = {
                OR = {
                    region = sr:region_balkans
                }
                state_region = {
                    is_homeland = cu:bosniak
                }
            }
        }
        random_scope_state = {
            limit = {
                region = sr:region_balkans
                state_region = {
                    is_homeland = cu:bosniak
                }
            }
            save_scope_as = bosnia_scope
        }
        create_country = {
            tag = BOS
            origin = root

            state = scope:bosnia_scope
        }
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            NOT = {
                exists = c:CRO
            }
            any_scope_state = {
                region = sr:region_balkans
                state_region = {
                    is_homeland = cu:croat
                }
            }
        }
        random_scope_state = {
            limit = {
                region = sr:region_balkans
                state_region = {
                    is_homeland = cu:croat
                }
            }
            save_scope_as = croatia_scope
        }
        create_country = {
            tag = CRO
            origin = root

            state = scope:croatia_scope
        }
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            NOT = {
                exists = c:MON
            }
            exists = s:STATE_MONTENEGRO.region_state:TUR
        }
        s:STATE_MONTENEGRO.region_state:TUR = {
            save_scope_as = montenegro_scope
        }
        create_country = {
            tag = MON
            origin = root

            state = scope:montenegro_scope
        }
        c:MON ?= {
            if = {
                    limit = {
                        sr:region_balkans = {
                            any_scope_state = {
                            state_region = {
                            is_homeland = cu:serb
                            }
                            owner = {
                                NOT = { this = root }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    NOT = {
                        has_journal_entry = je_spoils_of_war
                    }
                }
                add_journal_entry = {
                    type = je_spoils_of_war
                }
            }
        }
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            NOT = {
                exists = c:SER
            }
            any_scope_state = {
                region = sr:region_balkans
                state_region = {
                    is_homeland = cu:serb
                }
            }
        }
        random_scope_state = {
            limit = {
                region = sr:region_balkans
                state_region = {
                    is_homeland = cu:serb
                }
            }
            save_scope_as = serbia_scope
        }
        create_country = {
            tag = SER
            origin = root

            state = scope:serbia_scope
        }
        c:SER ?= {
            if = {
                    limit = {
                        sr:region_balkans = {
                            any_scope_state = {
                            state_region = {
                            is_homeland = cu:serb
                            }
                            owner = {
                                NOT = { this = root }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    NOT = {
                        has_journal_entry = je_spoils_of_war
                    }
                }
                add_journal_entry = {
                    type = je_spoils_of_war
                }
            }
        }
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            NOT = {
                exists = c:GRE
            }
            any_scope_state = {
                region = sr:region_balkans
                state_region = {
                    is_homeland = cu:greek
                }
            }
        }
        random_scope_state = {
            limit = {
                region = sr:region_balkans
                state_region = {
                    is_homeland = cu:greek
                }
            }
            save_scope_as = greece_scope
        }
        create_country = {
            tag = GRE
            origin = root

            state = scope:greece_scope
        }
        c:GRE ?= {
            if = {
                    limit = {
                        sr:region_balkans = {
                            any_scope_state = {
                            state_region = {
                            is_homeland = cu:greek
                            }
                            owner = {
                                NOT = { this = root }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    NOT = {
                        has_journal_entry = je_spoils_of_war
                    }
                }
                add_journal_entry = {
                    type = je_spoils_of_war
                }
            }
        }
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            NOT = {
                exists = c:WAL
            }
            exists = s:STATE_WALLACHIA.region_state:TUR
        }
        s:STATE_WALLACHIA.region_state:TUR = {
            save_scope_as = wallachia_scope
        }
        create_country = {
            tag = WAL
            origin = root

            state = scope:wallachia_scope
        }
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            NOT = {
                exists = c:MOL
            }
            exists = s:STATE_MOLDAVIA.region_state:TUR
        }
        s:STATE_MOLDAVIA.region_state:TUR = {
            save_scope_as = moldavia_scope
        }
        create_country = {
            tag = MOL
            origin = root

            state = scope:moldavia_scope
        }
    }

    # divide up states
    while = {
        limit = {
            any_scope_state = {
                region = sr:region_balkans
            }
        }
		random_country = {
			limit = {
                capital = {
                    region = sr:region_balkans
                }
				any_neighbouring_state = {
                    is_homeland_of_country_cultures = prev
					owner = root
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = new_owner_scope
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
                OR = {
                    region = sr:region_balkans
                }
                is_homeland_of_country_cultures = scope:new_owner_scope
				any_neighbouring_state = {
					owner = scope:new_owner_scope
				}
			}
			set_state_owner = scope:new_owner_scope
		}
		clear_saved_scope = new_owner_scope
    }

    if = {
        limit = {
            c:WAL ?= {
                is_direct_subject_of = ROOT
            }
        }
        c:WAL ?= {
            make_independent = yes
            add_journal_entry = {
                type = je_spoils_of_war
            }
        }
    }

    if = {
        limit = {
            c:MOL ?= {
                is_direct_subject_of = ROOT
            }
        }
        c:MOL ?= {
            make_independent = yes
        }
    }
}
﻿cuba_annex_effects = {
	active_law:lawgroup_slavery = {
		type = {
			save_temporary_scope_as = current_slave_law
		}
	}
	overlord ?= {
        save_temporary_scope_as = boss
        annex = c:CUB
        activate_law = scope:current_slave_law
        add_primary_culture = cu:caribeno
    }
    play_as = scope:boss
}

reapply_raiding_state_modifier = {
	remove_global_variable = mon_raid_state
	every_neighbouring_state = {
		limit = {
			has_modifier = raid_target
		}
		remove_modifier = raid_target
	}
	random_neighbouring_state = {
		limit = {
			owner ?= c:TUR
		}
		save_scope_as = raid_target
		if = {
			limit = {
				raiding_level >= 2
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = raid_target
				multiplier = raiding_level
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
			set_global_variable = {
				name = mon_raid_state
				value = scope:raid_target
			}
		}
		add_radicals_in_state = {
			value = {
				add = 0.015
				multiply = raiding_level
			}
		}
	}
}


end_raiding_cleanup = { #Used to #cleanup effects of je_montenegrin_raiding
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = montenegrin_raiding_stop_tt
		remove_modifier = modifier_montenegrin_raiding
		remove_modifier = modifier_montenegrin_disobedience
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		c:RUS ?= {
			remove_diplomatic_pact = {
				country = root
				type = disapproval_pact
			}
		}
		c:TUR ?= {
			remove_diplomatic_pact = {
				country = root
				type = raiding_pact
			}
		}
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		every_neighbouring_state = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = raid_target
			}
			remove_modifier = raid_target
		}
		c:TUR ?= {
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					has_modifier = raid_target
				}
				remove_modifier = raid_target
			}
		}
		remove_global_variable = mon_raid_state
	}
}

mon_failure_events = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_subject_of = c:TUR
			}
			trigger_event = {
				id = mon_state_formation.17
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = mon_are_barbarians
			}
			post_notification = je_mon_austria_alliance_fail
		}
		else = {
			trigger_event = {
				id = mon_state_formation.16
				popup = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

russia_breaks_independence_pact = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = c:MON
			c:RUS ?= {
				any_scope_treaty = {
					binds = c:MON
					any_scope_article = {
						has_type = guarantee_independence
					}
				}
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = russia_no_longer_guarantor_tt
			c:RUS ?= {
				every_scope_treaty = {
					limit = {
						binds = c:MON
						any_scope_article = {
							has_type = guarantee_independence
						}
					}
					withdraw = {
						country = c:RUS
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

increase_raiding = { #increases raiding intensity, if valid
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_journal_entry = je_montenegrin_raiding
			var:raiding_intensity < 100
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = mon_more_heads_tt
			change_variable = {
				name = raiding_intensity
				add = 25
			}
			remove_modifier = modifier_montenegrin_raiding
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_montenegrin_raiding
				multiplier = raiding_income
			}
			remove_modifier = modifier_montenegrin_disobedience
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_montenegrin_disobedience
				multiplier = var:raiding_intensity
			}
		}
	}
}

generate_ottoman_side_list = {
	clear_global_variable_list = ottoman_side_list
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = c:TUR
		}
		c:TUR ?= {
			add_to_global_variable_list = {
				name = ottoman_side_list
				target = THIS
			}
			every_country = { #add allies
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						c:TUR ?= THIS
					}
					OR = {
						is_subject_of = c:TUR
						any_scope_treaty = {
							binds = c:TUR
							any_scope_article = {
								OR = {
									has_type = alliance
									has_type = defensive_pact
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = potential_tur_ally
				##extra checks, this not also allied to Balkan League.
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {	#checking no already on list
							is_target_in_global_variable_list = {
								name = ottoman_side_list
								target = scope:potential_tur_ally
							}
						}
						NOT = {	#checking not on Balkan list
							is_target_in_global_variable_list = {
								name = balkan_league_side_list
								target = scope:potential_tur_ally
							}
						}
						NOT = { #not Balkan ally
							any_in_global_list = {
								variable = balkan_league_side_list
								save_temporary_scope_as = balkan_league_member
								any_scope_treaty = {
									binds = scope:potential_tur_ally
									any_scope_article = {
										OR = {
											has_type = alliance
											AND = {
												has_type = support_independence
												target_country = scope:balkan_league_member
											}
										}
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
					add_to_global_variable_list = {
						name = ottoman_side_list
						target = THIS
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

generate_balkan_league_side_list = {
	clear_global_variable_list = balkan_league_side_list
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			has_journal_entry = je_the_balkan_league
		}
		save_temporary_scope_as = balkan_league_member
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { #just in case already added
					is_target_in_global_variable_list = {
						name = balkan_league_side_list
						target = THIS
					}
				}
			}
			add_to_global_variable_list = {
				name = balkan_league_side_list
				target = THIS
			}
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				NOR = { #not above, not already counted
					this = scope:balkan_league_member
					is_target_in_global_variable_list = {
						name = balkan_league_side_list
						target = THIS
					}
				}
				any_scope_treaty = {
					binds = scope:balkan_league_member
					any_scope_article = {
						OR = {
							has_type = alliance
							AND = {
								has_type = support_independence
								target_country = scope:balkan_league_member
							}
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = { #not if also Turk ally
					any_scope_treaty = {
						binds = c:TUR
						any_scope_article = {
							OR = {
								has_type = alliance
								has_type = defensive_pact
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			add_to_global_variable_list = {
				name = balkan_league_side_list
				target = THIS
			}
		}
	}
}

transfer_province = { # Changes ownership of PROVINCE(here use ID number without p:, eg xF5F6C2) from GIVER to TAKER countries (e.g. c:TUR). Automatically checks GIVER owns the state.
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = $GIVER$
			exists = $TAKER$
			$TAKER$ != $GIVER$
			p:$PROVINCE$  = {
				$GIVER$ ?= state.owner
			}
		}
		p:$PROVINCE$.state.state_region = {
			set_owner_of_provinces = {
				country = $TAKER$
				provinces = { $PROVINCE$ }
			}
		}
	}
}

transfer_state = { # Changes ownership of any state within a STATE region (e.g. s:STATE_MONTENEGRO) from GIVER to TAKER countries (e.g. c:TUR). Automatically checks GIVER exists and owns the state.
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = $GIVER$
			exists = $TAKER$
			$TAKER$ != $GIVER$
			$STATE$ = {
				any_scope_state = {
					$GIVER$ = owner
				}
			}
		}
		$STATE$ = {
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					owner = $GIVER$
				}
				set_state_owner = $TAKER$
			}
		}
	}
}

balkan_war_give_tech = { # if c:TUR has $TECH$, and root doesn't, instant research it. Includes an else_if
	if = {
		limit = {
			c:TUR ?= {
				has_technology_researched = $TECH$
			}
			root = {
				NOT = {
					has_technology_researched = $TECH$
				}
			}
		}
		root = {
			add_technology_researched = $TECH$
			set_variable = {
				name = balkan_got_tech_cooldown
				years = 5
			}
		}
	}
}

destroy_barracks = { #Used to destroy and rebuild barracks in montenegrin_raiding.1 and montenegrin_raiding.2
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = mon_lost_men_tt
		save_temporary_scope_value_as = {
			name = barracks_amount
			value = barracks_levels_to_leave_unburned
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				state_region = s:STATE_MONTENEGRO
			}
			remove_building = building_barrack
			create_building = {
				building = "building_barrack"
				level = scope:barracks_amount
					reserves = 1
			}
		}
	}
}

eastern_crisis_2_event_effects = {
	c:TUR ?= {
		add_modifier = {
			name = eastern_crisis_controlled
			days = moderately_long_modifier_time
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				is_subject_of = root
				OR = {
					capital.region = sr:region_balkans
					has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_balkans
				}
			}
			c:TUR.power_bloc ?= {
				add_leverage = { target = PREV value = 250 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_pop = {
					culture = cu:albanian
				}
			}
			add_fervor_target_modifier = { #changes target
				culture = cu:albanian
				days = very_long_modifier_time
				multiplier = eastern_crisis_fervor_loss
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_pop = {
					culture = cu:bosniak
				}
			}
			add_fervor_target_modifier = { #changes target
				culture = cu:bosniak
				days = very_long_modifier_time
				multiplier = eastern_crisis_fervor_loss
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_pop = {
					culture = cu:bulgarian
				}
			}
			add_fervor_target_modifier = { #changes target
				culture = cu:bulgarian
				days = very_long_modifier_time
				multiplier = eastern_crisis_fervor_loss
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_pop = {
					culture = cu:croat
				}
			}
			add_fervor_target_modifier = { #changes target
				culture = cu:croat
				days = very_long_modifier_time
				multiplier = eastern_crisis_fervor_loss
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_pop = {
					culture = cu:greek
				}
			}
			add_fervor_target_modifier = { #changes target
				culture = cu:greek
				days = very_long_modifier_time
				multiplier = eastern_crisis_fervor_loss
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_pop = {
					culture = cu:romanian
				}
			}
			add_fervor_target_modifier = { #changes target
				culture = cu:romanian
				days = very_long_modifier_time
				multiplier = eastern_crisis_fervor_loss
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_pop = {
					culture = cu:serb
				}
			}
			add_fervor_target_modifier = { #changes target
				culture = cu:serb
				days = very_long_modifier_time
				multiplier = eastern_crisis_fervor_loss
			}
		}
	}
}

eastern_crisis_3_event_effects = {
	c:TUR ?= {
		add_modifier = {
			name = eastern_crisis_not_controlled
			days = moderately_long_modifier_time
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_country = {
					OR = {
						is_subject_of = root
						capital.region = sr:region_balkans
					}
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = eastern_crisis_subjects_modifier__ten_years_tt
			}
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_subject_of = root
						capital.region = sr:region_balkans
					}
				}
				hidden_effect = { #covered by custom tooltip above
					add_modifier = {
						name = eastern_crisis_liberty_desire_increase
						days = long_modifier_time
						is_decaying = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}

		if = { #lose leverage
			limit = {
				exists = c:TUR.power_bloc
			}
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					capital.region = sr:region_balkans
				}
				c:TUR.power_bloc ?= {
					add_leverage = { target = PREV value = -500 }
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				any_political_movement = {
					OR = {
						is_political_movement_type = movement_religious_majority
						is_political_movement_type = movement_religious_minority
						is_political_movement_type = movement_cultural_majority
						is_political_movement_type = movement_cultural_minority
					}
				}
			}
			every_political_movement = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_political_movement_type = movement_religious_majority
						is_political_movement_type = movement_religious_minority
						is_political_movement_type = movement_cultural_majority
						is_political_movement_type = movement_cultural_minority
					}
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = eastern_crisis_movement_attraction
					days = long_modifier_time
					is_decaying = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_pop = {
					culture = cu:albanian
				}
			}
			add_fervor_target_modifier = { #changes target
				culture = cu:albanian
				multiplier = eastern_crisis_fervor_gain
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_pop = {
					culture = cu:bosniak
				}
			}
			add_fervor_target_modifier = { #changes target
				culture = cu:bosniak
				multiplier = eastern_crisis_fervor_gain
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_pop = {
					culture = cu:bulgarian
				}
			}
			add_fervor_target_modifier = { #changes target
				culture = cu:bulgarian
				multiplier = eastern_crisis_fervor_gain
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_pop = {
					culture = cu:croat
				}
			}
			add_fervor_target_modifier = { #changes target
				culture = cu:croat
				multiplier = eastern_crisis_fervor_gain
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_pop = {
					culture = cu:greek
				}
			}
			add_fervor_target_modifier = { #changes target
				culture = cu:greek
				multiplier = eastern_crisis_fervor_gain
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_pop = {
					culture = cu:romanian
				}
			}
			add_fervor_target_modifier = { #changes target
				culture = cu:romanian
				multiplier = eastern_crisis_fervor_gain
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_pop = {
					culture = cu:serb
				}
			}
			add_fervor_target_modifier = { #changes target
				culture = cu:serb
				multiplier = eastern_crisis_fervor_gain
			}
		}
	}

﻿# All dynamic names should be applied on a strategic region level for ease of use and to avoid overlapping script
evaluate_and_assign_state_hub_dynamic_names = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_game_rule = no_dynamic_naming
		}
		reset_state_name = yes
	    reset_hub_names = yes
	}
	else = {
		assign_state_hub_dynamic_names = yes
	}
}

    }

assign_state_name_region_congo  = {

        limit = {
            state_region = {
                s:STATE_BAS_CONGO ?= this
            }
        }
        STATE_BAS_CONGO_state_name_assign = yes
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            state_region = {
                s:STATE_CONGO ?= this
            }
        }
        STATE_CONGO_state_name_assign = yes
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            state_region = {
                s:STATE_CONGO_ORIENTALE ?= this
            }
        }
        STATE_CONGO_ORIENTALE_state_name_assign = yes
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            state_region = {
                s:STATE_SOUTH_CAMEROON ?= this
            }
        }
        STATE_SOUTH_CAMEROON_state_name_assign = yes
    }
}

#######################################
### Specific state name assignments ###
#######################################

# North Africa Region

STATE_TUNISIA_state_name_assign = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait = language_italian }
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_TUNISIA_italian
        set_hub_names = italian

    }
    else = { # By putting an else at the end that resets the names we ensure that the names are switched back to their defaults if anyone else takes the states
        reset_state_name = yes
        reset_hub_names = yes
    }
}

# Niger Region

STATE_NORTH_CAMEROON_state_name_assign = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait = language_germanophone }
                NOT = { country_has_primary_culture = cu:french }
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_NORTH_CAMEROON_german

        set_hub_name = {
            type = city
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_NORTH_CAMEROON_city_german
        }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                NOT = { any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait = language_germanophone } }
                country_has_primary_culture = cu:french
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_NORTH_CAMEROON_french
    }
    else = { # By putting an else at the end that resets the names we ensure that the names are switched back to their defaults if anyone else takes the states
        reset_state_name = yes
        reset_hub_names = yes
    }
}

# Congo Region

STATE_BAS_CONGO_state_name_assign = {
	if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                OR = {
                    c:BEL ?= this
                    AND = {
                        exists = top_overlord
                        exists = c:BEL
                        top_overlord = c:BEL
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_BAS_CONGO_french
        set_hub_names = french

    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                OR = {
                    country_has_primary_culture = cu:dutch
                    country_has_primary_culture = cu:flemish
                    country_has_primary_culture = cu:boer
                    AND = {
                        c:BEL ?= this
                        NOT = {
                            any_scope_state = {
                                state_region = s:STATE_WALLONIA
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_BAS_CONGO_dutch

        set_hub_name = {
            type = city
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_BAS_CONGO_city_dutch
        }
    }
	else = { # By putting an else at the end that resets the names we ensure that the names are switched back to their defaults if anyone else takes the states
		reset_state_name = yes
		reset_hub_names = yes
	}
}

STATE_CONGO_state_name_assign = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
                    has_discrimination_trait = language_francophone
                }
            }
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = city
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_CONGO_city_french
        }
        set_hub_name = {
            type = port
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_CONGO_port_french
        }
        set_hub_name = {
            type = farm
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_CONGO_farm_french
        }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
                    has_discrimination_trait = language_lusophone
                }
            }
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = port
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_CONGO_port_portuguese
        }
    }
    else = { # By putting an else at the end that resets the names we ensure that the names are switched back to their defaults if anyone else takes the states
        reset_state_name = yes
        reset_hub_names = yes
    }
}

STATE_SOUTH_CAMEROON_state_name_assign = {
    if = {
        # You only own Fernando Po
        limit = {
            owner = {
                p:x902089.state.owner = this
                p:x902089.state = {
                    NOT = {
                        num_provinces > 1
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = {
                owner = {
                    any_primary_culture = {
                        has_discrimination_trait = language_hispanophone
                    }
                }
            }
            set_state_name = STATE_SOUTH_CAMEROON_fernando_po_spanish
            set_hub_name = {
                type = farm
                name = HUB_NAME_STATE_SOUTH_CAMEROON_farm_spanish
            }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                owner = {
                    any_primary_culture = {
                        has_discrimination_trait = language_anglophone
                    }
                }
            }
            set_state_name = STATE_SOUTH_CAMEROON_fernando_po_english
            set_hub_name = {
                type = farm
                name = HUB_NAME_STATE_SOUTH_CAMEROON_farm_english
            }
        }
    }
    else_if = {
        # You only own Sao Tome
        limit = {
            owner = {
                p:xC02050.state.owner = this
                p:xC02050.state = {
                    NOT = {
                        num_provinces > 1
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = {
                owner = {
                    any_primary_culture = {
                        has_discrimination_trait = language_lusophone
                    }
                }
            }
            set_state_name = STATE_SOUTH_CAMEROON_sao_tome_portuguese
        }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait = language_germanophone }
                NOT = { country_has_primary_culture = cu:french }
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_SOUTH_CAMEROON_german

        set_hub_name = {
            type = city
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_SOUTH_CAMEROON_city_german
        }
        set_hub_name = {
            type = port
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_SOUTH_CAMEROON_port_german
        }
        set_hub_name = {
            type = mine
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_SOUTH_CAMEROON_mine_german
        }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                NOT = { any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait = language_germanophone } }
                any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait = language_francophone }
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_SOUTH_CAMEROON_french

        set_hub_name = {
            type = city
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_SOUTH_CAMEROON_city_french
        }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
                    has_discrimination_trait = language_lusophone
                }
            }
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = port
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_SOUTH_CAMEROON_port_portuguese
        }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
                    has_discrimination_trait = language_hispanophone
                }
            }
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = farm
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_SOUTH_CAMEROON_farm_spanish
        }
    }
    else = { # By putting an else at the end that resets the names we ensure that the names are switched back to their defaults if anyone else takes the states
        reset_state_name = yes
        reset_hub_names = yes
    }
}

STATE_CONGO_ORIENTALE_state_name_assign = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                OR = {
                    c:BEL ?= this
                    any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait = language_francophone }
                }
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_CONGO_ORIENTALE_bel

        set_hub_name = {
            type = city
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_CONGO_ORIENTALE_city_bel
        }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                OR = {
                    country_has_primary_culture = cu:dutch
                    country_has_primary_culture = cu:flemish
                    country_has_primary_culture = cu:boer
                    AND = {
                        c:BEL ?= this
                        NOT = {
                            any_scope_state = {
                                state_region = s:STATE_WALLONIA
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_CONGO_ORIENTALE_dutch

        set_hub_name = {
            type = city
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_CONGO_ORIENTALE_city_dutch
        }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait = language_anglophone }
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_CONGO_ORIENTALE_uk

        set_hub_name = {
            type = city
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_CONGO_ORIENTALE_city_uk
        }
    }
    else = { # By putting an else at the end that resets the names we ensure that the names are switched back to their defaults if anyone else takes the states
        reset_state_name = yes
        reset_hub_names = yes
    }
}

STATE_ZULULAND_state_name_assign = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                NOT = { country_has_primary_culture = cu:british }
                any_primary_culture = { 
                    has_discrimination_trait = language_dutch 
                }
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_ZULULAND_boer

        set_hub_names = boer
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                OR = {
                    c:GBR ?= this
                    c:SAF ?= this
                    AND = {
                        exists = c:GBR
                        exists = top_overlord
                        top_overlord = c:GBR
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_ZULULAND_boer
        set_hub_names = british
    }
    else = { # By putting an else at the end that resets the names we ensure that the names are switched back to their defaults if anyone else takes the states
        reset_state_name = yes
        reset_hub_names = yes
    }
}

﻿# Culture name assignments
### REGION TRIGGERS ###

assign_state_name_region_the_midwest = { # region_atlantic_coast
    if = {
        limit = {
            state_region = {
                s:STATE_OHIO ?= this
            }
        }
        STATE_OHIO_state_name_assign = yes
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            state_region = {
                s:STATE_WASHINGTON ?= this
            }
        }
        STATE_WASHINGTON_state_name_assign = yes
    }
}

assign_state_name_region_canada = { # region_canada
    if = {
        limit = {
            state_region = {
                s:STATE_ONTARIO ?= this
            }
        }
        STATE_ONTARIO_state_name_assign = yes
    }
}

    }

#######################################
### Specific state name assignments ###
#######################################

STATE_OHIO_state_name_assign = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                c:UOM ?= this
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_OHIO_michigander
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                country_has_primary_culture = cu:japanese
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_OHIO_japanese
    }
    else = { # switch back to defaults
        reset_state_name = yes
        reset_hub_names = yes
    }
}

STATE_WASHINGTON_state_name_assign = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			owner = {
				c:USA ?= this
			}
		}

        set_state_name = STATE_WASHINGTON_usa

        set_hub_name = {
            type = city
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_WASHINGTON_city_usa
        }
	}
	# no name reversion effect in an else statement. If the state becomes Washington, it stays Washington.
}

STATE_BRITISH_COLUMBIA_state_name_assign = { # More likely that British Columbia gets its name from conclusion of Oregon JE, but this serves as a one-off fallback
	if = {
		limit = {
			owner = {
				OR = {
					c:GBR ?= this
					is_subject_of = c:GBR
					any_primary_culture = {
						OR = {
							cu:anglo_canadian = this
							cu:franco_canadian = this
						}
					}
				}
			}
			s:STATE_WASHINGTON = {
				any_scope_state = {
					owner = {
						c:USA ?= this
					}
				}
			}
		}

        set_state_name = STATE_BRITISH_COLUMBIA_british
	}
	# no name reversion effect in an else statement. If the state becomes British Columbia, it stays British Columbia.
}

STATE_ONTARIO_state_name_assign = {	# this effect is also covered by completing je_canada_can or je_canada_gbr but I am redundantly covering all bases in a redundant manner
	if = {
		limit = {
			owner = {
				OR = {
					country_has_primary_culture = cu:anglo_canadian
					country_has_primary_culture = cu:franco_canadian
				}
				has_technology_researched = nationalism
			}
		}

        set_hub_name = {
            type = city
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_ONTARIO_city_ottawa
        }
	}
	# no name reversion effect in an else statement. If the city hub becomes Ottawa, it stays Ottawa
}

STATE_DISTRICT_OF_COLUMBIA_state_name_assign = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                OR = {
                    c:GBR ?= this
                    AND = {
                        exists = top_overlord
                        exists = c:GBR
                        NOT = { c:USA ?= this }
                        top_overlord = c:GBR
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = city
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_DISTRICT_OF_COLUMBIA_city_uk
        }
    }
    else = { # switch back to defaults
        reset_state_name = yes
        reset_hub_names = yes
    }
}

STATE_SANTA_CATARINA_state_name_assign = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                c:BRZ ?= this
                OR = {
                    has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_presidential_republic
                    has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_parliamentary_republic
                }
            }
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = port
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_SANTA_CATARINA_port_bra_republic
        }
    }
    else = { # switch back to defaults
        reset_state_name = yes
        reset_hub_names = yes
    }
}

STATE_HAITI_state_name_assign = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                c:HAI ?= this
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = {
                owner = {
                    any_scope_state = {
                        state_region = s:STATE_SANTO_DOMINGO
                    }
                }
            }
            set_state_name = STATE_HAITI_french_haiti_whole
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                owner = {
                    NOT = {
                        any_scope_state = {
                            state_region = s:STATE_SANTO_DOMINGO
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            set_state_name = STATE_HAITI_french_haiti_part
        }

        set_hub_names = french_haiti
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
                    has_discrimination_trait = language_francophone
                }
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = {
                owner = {
                    any_scope_state = {
                        state_region = s:STATE_SANTO_DOMINGO
                    }
                }
            }
            set_state_name = STATE_HAITI_french_whole
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                owner = {
                    NOT = {
                        any_scope_state = {
                            state_region = s:STATE_SANTO_DOMINGO
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            set_state_name = STATE_HAITI_french_part
        }

        set_hub_names = french
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
                    has_discrimination_trait = language_hispanophone
                }
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = {
                owner = {
                    any_scope_state = {
                        state_region = s:STATE_SANTO_DOMINGO
                    }
                }
            }
            set_state_name = STATE_HAITI_spanish_whole
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                owner = {
                    NOT = {
                        any_scope_state = {
                            state_region = s:STATE_SANTO_DOMINGO
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            set_state_name = STATE_HAITI_spanish_part
        }

        set_hub_names = spanish
    }
    else = { # switch back to defaults
        reset_state_name = yes
        reset_hub_names = yes
    }
}

    }

        STATE_HOKUSHINETSU_state_name_assign = yes

#######################################

## Anatolia Region
# East Aegean Islands
STATE_EAST_AEGEAN_ISLANDS_state_name_assign = {
    if = { # Greek
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
                    has_discrimination_trait = language_greek
                }
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_EAST_AEGEAN_ISLANDS_greek

        set_hub_name = {
            type = city
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EAST_AEGEAN_ISLANDS_city_greek
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = farm
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EAST_AEGEAN_ISLANDS_farm_greek
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = wood
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EAST_AEGEAN_ISLANDS_wood_greek
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = port
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EAST_AEGEAN_ISLANDS_port_greek
        }
    }
    else_if = { # Turkish
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
                    has_discrimination_trait_group = language_group_turkic
                }
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_EAST_AEGEAN_ISLANDS_turkish

        set_hub_name = {
            type = city
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EAST_AEGEAN_ISLANDS_city_turkish
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = farm
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EAST_AEGEAN_ISLANDS_farm_turkish
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = wood
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EAST_AEGEAN_ISLANDS_wood_turkish
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = port
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EAST_AEGEAN_ISLANDS_port_turkish
        }
    }
    else = { # By putting an else at the end that resets the names we ensure that the names are switched back to their defaults if anyone else takes the states
        reset_state_name = yes
        reset_hub_names = yes
    }
}

# Eastern Thrace
STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_state_name_assign = {
    if = { # Greek
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
                    has_discrimination_trait = language_greek
                }
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_greek

        set_hub_name = {
            type = city
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_city_greek
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = farm
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_farm_greek
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = wood
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_wood_greek
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = mine
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_mine_greek
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = port
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_port_greek
        }
    }
    else_if = { # Imperial Russia (Autocratic Empire)
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
                    cu:russian ?= this
                }
                has_law = law_type:law_monarchy
                country_has_voting_franchise = no
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_russian

        set_hub_name = {
            type = city
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_city_russian_tsargrad # Tsargrad
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = farm
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_farm_russian
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = wood
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_wood_russian
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = mine
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_mine_russian
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = port
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_port_russian
        }
    }
    else_if = { # Imperial Russia
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
                    cu:russian ?= this
                }
                NOT = {
                    has_law = law_type:law_monarchy
                }
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_russian

        set_hub_name = {
            type = city
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_city_russian # Konstantinopol
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = farm
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_farm_russian
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = wood
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_wood_russian
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = mine
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_mine_russian
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = port
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_port_russian
        }
    }
    else_if = { # Turkish
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
                    has_discrimination_trait_group = language_group_turkic
                }
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_turkish

        set_hub_name = {
            type = city
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_city_turkish
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = farm
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_farm_turkish
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = wood
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_wood_turkish
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = mine
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_mine_turkish
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = port
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_port_turkish
        }
    }
    else_if = { # Bulgarian Monarchy
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
                    has_discrimination_trait = language_bulgarian
                }
                has_law = law_type:law_monarchy
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_bulgarian

        set_hub_name = {
            type = city
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_city_bulgarian_tsarigrad
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = farm
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_farm_bulgarian
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = wood
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_wood_bulgarian
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = mine
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_mine_bulgarian
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = port
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_port_bulgarian
        }
    }
    else_if = { # Bulgarian Non-Monarchy
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
                    has_discrimination_trait = language_bulgarian
                }
                NOT = {
                    has_law = law_type:law_monarchy
                }
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_bulgarian

        set_hub_name = {
            type = city
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_city_bulgarian
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = farm
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_farm_bulgarian
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = wood
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_wood_bulgarian
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = mine
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_mine_bulgarian
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = port
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_EASTERN_THRACE_port_bulgarian
        }
    }
    else = { # By putting an else at the end that resets the names we ensure that the names are switched back to their defaults if anyone else takes the states
        reset_state_name = yes
        reset_hub_names = yes
    }
}

STATE_SAKHALIN_state_name_assign = { # This looks pretty grim, but is necessary since you can only scope from province to state, and not the other way around.
    if = {
        limit = { # You own both Kuril and Sakhalin provinces
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
                    has_discrimination_trait = language_east_slavic
                }
                AND = {
                    OR = { # Kuril Islands
                        p:x1E5261.state.owner = this
                        p:x5B9D2D.state.owner = this
                        p:x7F67EF.state.owner = this
                        p:x601140.state.owner = this
                        p:xA060DF.state.owner = this
                    }
                    OR = { # Sakhalin Island
                        p:xF1B27F.state.owner = this
                        p:x041FF2.state.owner = this
                        p:x9628A0.state.owner = this
                        p:x4ED0E9.state.owner = this
                        p:x768145.state.owner = this
                        p:x1B4E8D.state.owner = this
                        p:x1CA52F.state.owner = this
                        p:xC3C06E.state.owner = this
                        p:x22C190.state.owner = this
                        p:xB8277E.state.owner = this
                        p:x503660.state.owner = this
                        p:x02BAB7.state.owner = this
                        p:x2010C0.state.owner = this
                        p:x9E3A0A.state.owner = this
                        p:x2492AA.state.owner = this
                        p:xD0294D.state.owner = this
                        p:x47B65C.state.owner = this
                        p:xE99CEA.state.owner = this
                        p:xA5E421.state.owner = this
                        p:x3405B2.state.owner = this
                        p:xBE16C2.state.owner = this
                        p:xF15B61.state.owner = this
                        p:xDFA060.state.owner = this
                        p:x7608FE.state.owner = this
                        p:x1CAAE0.state.owner = this
                        p:xEAFA0A.state.owner = this
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_SAKHALIN_russian_sakhalin_and_kuril
        set_hub_names = russian
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { # You own Sakhalin provinces, but no Kuril provinces
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
                    has_discrimination_trait = language_east_slavic
                }
                AND = {
                    NOR = { # Kuril Islands
                        p:x1E5261.state.owner = this
                        p:x5B9D2D.state.owner = this
                        p:x7F67EF.state.owner = this
                        p:x601140.state.owner = this
                        p:xA060DF.state.owner = this
                    }
                    OR = { # Sakhalin Island
                        p:xF1B27F.state.owner = this
                        p:x041FF2.state.owner = this
                        p:x9628A0.state.owner = this
                        p:x4ED0E9.state.owner = this
                        p:x768145.state.owner = this
                        p:x1B4E8D.state.owner = this
                        p:x1CA52F.state.owner = this
                        p:xC3C06E.state.owner = this
                        p:x22C190.state.owner = this
                        p:xB8277E.state.owner = this
                        p:x503660.state.owner = this
                        p:x02BAB7.state.owner = this
                        p:x2010C0.state.owner = this
                        p:x9E3A0A.state.owner = this
                        p:x2492AA.state.owner = this
                        p:xD0294D.state.owner = this
                        p:x47B65C.state.owner = this
                        p:xE99CEA.state.owner = this
                        p:xA5E421.state.owner = this
                        p:x3405B2.state.owner = this
                        p:xBE16C2.state.owner = this
                        p:xF15B61.state.owner = this
                        p:xDFA060.state.owner = this
                        p:x7608FE.state.owner = this
                        p:x1CAAE0.state.owner = this
                        p:xEAFA0A.state.owner = this
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_SAKHALIN_russian_sakhalin_only
        set_hub_names = russian
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { # You own Kuril provinces, but no Sakhalin provinces
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
                    has_discrimination_trait = language_east_slavic
                }
                AND = {
                    OR = { # Kuril Islands
                        p:x1E5261.state.owner = this
                        p:x5B9D2D.state.owner = this
                        p:x7F67EF.state.owner = this
                        p:x601140.state.owner = this
                        p:xA060DF.state.owner = this

                    NOR = { # Sakhalin Island
                        p:xF1B27F.state.owner = this

                    }
                }
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_SAKHALIN_russian_kuril_only
        set_hub_names = russian
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { # You own both Kuril and Sakhalin provinces

STATE_HOKKAIDO_state_name_assign = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
                    OR = {
                        has_discrimination_trait = language_ainu
                        has_discrimination_trait = language_siberian
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_HOKKAIDO_ainu
        set_hub_names = ainu
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
                    has_discrimination_trait = language_east_slavic
                }
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_HOKKAIDO_russian
        set_hub_names = russian
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
                    has_discrimination_trait = language_japanese
                }
                NOT = {
                    any_primary_culture = {
                        has_discrimination_trait = language_ainu
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        if = {
            limit = {
                has_global_variable = hokkaido_name_1_global_var
            }
            set_state_name = STATE_HOKKAIDO_japanese_hokkaido
        }

        else_if = {
            limit = {
                has_global_variable = hokkaido_name_2_global_var
            }
            set_state_name = STATE_HOKKAIDO_japanese_hokkaido
        }

        else_if = {
            limit = {
                has_global_variable = hokkaido_name_3_global_var
            }
            set_state_name = STATE_HOKKAIDO_japanese_kaitodo
        }

        else_if = {
            limit = {
                has_global_variable = hokkaido_name_4_global_var
            }
            set_state_name = STATE_HOKKAIDO_japanese_hitakamido
        }

        else = {
            set_state_name = STATE_HOKKAIDO_japanese
        }

        set_hub_names = japanese

        if = {
            limit = {
                has_global_variable = sapporo_founded_global_var
            }
            set_hub_name = {
                type = city
                name = HUB_NAME_STATE_HOKKAIDO_city_sapporo
            }
        }
    }
    else = {
        reset_state_name = yes
        reset_hub_names = yes
    }
}

## Indonesia Region
# West Java
STATE_WEST_JAVA_state_name_assign = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                NOT = {
                    any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_european } # If Britain took the city, the city would still be called Batavia, but if Japan took it they'd call it Jakarta
                }
            }
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = city
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_WEST_JAVA_city_jakarta
        }
    }
    else = {
        reset_state_name = yes
        reset_hub_names = yes
    }
}

## Madras Region
# Kurnool
STATE_KURNOOL_state_name_assign = {
    if = { # South Asian
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
                    has_discrimination_trait_group = heritage_group_south_asian
                }
                NOT = {
                    any_overlord_or_above = {
                        any_primary_culture = {
                            has_discrimination_trait = language_anglophone
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_KURNOOL_indian

        set_hub_name = {
            type = city
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_KURNOOL_city_indian
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = farm
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_KURNOOL_farm_indian
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = wood
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_KURNOOL_wood_indian
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = mine
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_KURNOOL_mine_indian
        }
    }
    else = { # By putting an else at the end that resets the names we ensure that the names are switched back to their defaults if anyone else takes the states
        reset_state_name = yes
        reset_hub_names = yes
    }
}

            owner = {
                country_has_primary_culture = cu:vietnamese
            }
        }
        set_state_name = STATE_ANNAM_vietnamese
    }
    else_if = { # French
        limit = {
            owner = {
                country_has_primary_culture = cu:french
            }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = port
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_ANNAM_port_french
        }
    }
    else = { # By putting an else at the end that resets the names we ensure that the names are switched back to their defaults if anyone else takes the states

# Cambodia
STATE_CAMBODIA_state_name_assign = {
    if = { # Khmer
        limit = {
            owner = {
                country_has_primary_culture = cu:khmer
            }
        }
        set_state_name = STATE_CAMBODIA_khmer
    }
    else_if = { # Vietnamese
        limit = {
            owner = {
                country_has_primary_culture = cu:vietnamese
                has_law = law_type:law_monarchy
            }
        }
        set_state_name = STATE_CAMBODIA_vietnamese_monarchy

        set_hub_name = {
            type = city
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_CAMBODIA_city_vietnamese
        }
    }
    else_if = { # French
        limit = {
            owner = {
                country_has_primary_culture = cu:french
            }
        }
        set_state_name = STATE_CAMBODIA_french
    }
    else = { # By putting an else at the end that resets the names we ensure that the names are switched back to their defaults if anyone else takes the states
        reset_state_name = yes
        reset_hub_names = yes
    }
}

﻿### Culture name assignments
# Dnieper Region
assign_state_name_region_dnieper  = {
	if = {
        limit = {
            state_region = {
                s:STATE_KIEV ?= this
            }
        }
        STATE_KIEV_state_name_assign = yes
    }
	if = {
        limit = {
            state_region = {
                s:STATE_VOLHYNIA ?= this
            }
        }
        STATE_VOLHYNIA_state_name_assign = yes
    }
	if = {
        limit = {
            state_region = {
                s:STATE_CHERSON ?= this
            }
        }
        STATE_CHERSON_state_name_assign = yes
    }
	if = {
        limit = {
            state_region = {
                s:STATE_KHARKOV ?= this
            }
        }
        STATE_KHARKOV_state_name_assign = yes
    }
	if = {
        limit = {
            state_region = {
                s:STATE_TAURIDA ?= this
            }
        }
        STATE_TAURIDA_state_name_assign = yes
    }
	if = {
        limit = {
            state_region = {
                s:STATE_CRIMEA ?= this
            }
        }
        STATE_CRIMEA_state_name_assign = yes
    }
	if = {
        limit = {
            state_region = {
                s:STATE_CHERNIHIV ?= this
            }
        }
        STATE_CHERNIHIV_state_name_assign = yes
    }
}

#######################################
### Specific state name assignments ###
#######################################

# Dnieper Region

STATE_KIEV_state_name_assign = {
	if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
                    cu:russian ?= this
                }
				NOT = {
					any_primary_culture = {
						cu:ukrainian ?= this
					}
				}
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_KIEV_russian

        set_hub_name = {
            type = city
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_KIEV_city_russian
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = farm
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_KIEV_farm_russian
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = wood
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_KIEV_wood_russian
        }
    }
	else = { # By putting an else at the end that resets the names we ensure that the names are switched back to their defaults if anyone else takes the states
		reset_state_name = yes
		reset_hub_names = yes
	}
}

STATE_VOLHYNIA_state_name_assign = {
	if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
                    cu:russian ?= this
                }
				NOT = {
					any_primary_culture = {
						cu:ukrainian ?= this
					}
				}
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_VOLHYNIA_russian
        set_hub_names = russian
    }
	else = { # By putting an else at the end that resets the names we ensure that the names are switched back to their defaults if anyone else takes the states
		reset_state_name = yes
		reset_hub_names = yes
	}
}

STATE_CHERSON_state_name_assign = {
	if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
                    cu:russian ?= this
                }
				NOT = {
					any_primary_culture = {
						cu:ukrainian ?= this
					}
				}
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_CHERSON_russian
        set_hub_names = russian
    }
	else = { # By putting an else at the end that resets the names we ensure that the names are switched back to their defaults if anyone else takes the states
		reset_state_name = yes
		reset_hub_names = yes
	}
}

STATE_LOWER_SILESIA_state_name_assign = {
	if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
					cu:polish ?= this
                }
				NOT = {
					any_primary_culture = {
						has_discrimination_trait = language_germanophone
					}
				}
            }
        }
        set_state_name = STATE_LOWER_SILESIA_polish
        set_hub_names = polish
    }
	else_if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
					OR = {
						cu:north_german ?= this
						cu:south_german ?= this
					}
                }
				NOT = {
					any_primary_culture = {
						cu:polish ?= this
					}
				}
            }
        }
        set_state_name = STATE_LOWER_SILESIA_german
		reset_hub_names = yes # Hubs are German by default
    }
	else_if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
					cu:czech ?= this
                }
            }
        }
        set_state_name = STATE_LOWER_SILESIA_czech
        set_hub_names = czech
    }
	else = { # By putting an else at the end that resets the names we ensure that the names are switched back to their defaults if anyone else takes the states
		reset_state_name = yes
		reset_hub_names = yes
	}
}

STATE_UPPER_SILESIA_state_name_assign = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
                    cu:polish ?= this
                }
                NOT = {
                    any_primary_culture = {
                        has_discrimination_trait = language_germanophone
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        set_state_name = STATE_UPPER_SILESIA_polish
        set_hub_names = polish
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                c:AUS ?= this
                any_primary_culture = {
                    OR = {
                        cu:north_german ?= this
                        cu:south_german ?= this
                    }
                }
                NOT = {
                    owns_entire_state_region = STATE_UPPER_SILESIA
                }
            }
        }
        set_state_name = STATE_UPPER_SILESIA_german_austrian
        reset_hub_names = yes # Hubs are German by default
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
                    OR = {
                        cu:north_german ?= this
                        cu:south_german ?= this
                    }
                }
                NOT = {
                    any_primary_culture = {
                        cu:polish ?= this
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        set_state_name = STATE_UPPER_SILESIA_german
        reset_hub_names = yes # Hubs are German by default
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
                    cu:czech ?= this
                }
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = {
                is_split_state = yes
            }
            set_state_name = STATE_UPPER_SILESIA_czech_split
        }
        else = {
            set_state_name = STATE_UPPER_SILESIA_czech
        }
        set_hub_names = czech
    }
    else = { # By putting an else at the end that resets the names we ensure that the names are switched back to their defaults if anyone else takes the states
        reset_state_name = yes
        reset_hub_names = yes
    }
}

# Finland Region

STATE_KOLA_state_name_assign = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			owner = {
				any_primary_culture = {
					cu:russian ?= this
				}
			}
		}

		# By not putting an else at the end and doing the resetting for a specific culture the name will stay whatever it was if taken by someone other than the scripted cultures
		reset_state_name = yes
		reset_hub_names = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			owner = {
				any_primary_culture = {
					cu:finnish ?= this
				}
			}
		}

		set_state_name = STATE_KOLA_finnish
		set_hub_names = finnish
	}
}

## Balkans Region
# Slovenia
STATE_SLOVENIA_state_name_assign = {
	if = { # German
        limit = {
            owner = {
            	any_primary_culture = {
            		has_discrimination_trait = language_germanophone
            	}
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_SLOVENIA_german # Carniola made up the majority of modern day Slovenia, but it's not a perfect fit.

        set_hub_name = {
            type = city
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_SLOVENIA_city_german
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = farm
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_SLOVENIA_farm_german
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = wood
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_SLOVENIA_wood_german
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = mine
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_SLOVENIA_mine_german
        }
    }
	else_if = { # Italian
        limit = {
            owner = {
            	any_primary_culture = {
            		has_discrimination_trait = language_italian
            	}
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_SLOVENIA_italian # Carniola made up the majority of modern day Slovenia, but it's not a perfect fit.

        set_hub_name = {
            type = city
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_SLOVENIA_city_italian
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = farm

# Central Hungary
STATE_CENTRAL_HUNGARY_state_name_assign = {
    if = { # Hungarian
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
                    has_discrimination_trait = language_hungarian
                }
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_CENTRAL_HUNGARY_hungarian

        if = {
            limit = {
                has_global_variable = budapest_founded_global_var
            }
            set_hub_name = {
                type = city
                name = HUB_NAME_STATE_CENTRAL_HUNGARY_city_hungarian_budapest
            }
        }
        else = {
            set_hub_name = {
                type = city
                name = HUB_NAME_STATE_CENTRAL_HUNGARY_city_hungarian
            }
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = farm
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_CENTRAL_HUNGARY_farm_hungarian
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = wood
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_CENTRAL_HUNGARY_wood_hungarian
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = mine
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_CENTRAL_HUNGARY_mine_hungarian
        }
    }

                any_primary_culture = {
                    has_discrimination_trait = language_germanophone
                }
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_CENTRAL_HUNGARY_german

## Rhine
STATE_FLANDERS_state_name_assign = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			owner = {
				any_primary_culture = {
					cu:french ?= this
				}
				NOT = { c:HRE ?= this } # I don't want to even think about how that would work
			}
		}

        set_state_name = STATE_FLANDERS_french
        set_hub_names = french

	}
    if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
                    has_discrimination_trait = language_germanophone
                }
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_FLANDERS_german
        set_hub_names = german

    }
	else = { # switch back to defaults
		reset_state_name = yes
		reset_hub_names = yes
	}
}

STATE_WALLONIA_state_name_assign = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			owner = {
				any_primary_culture = {
					cu:french ?= this
				}
				NOT = { c:HRE ?= this } # I don't want to even think about how that would work
			}
		}

        set_state_name = STATE_WALLONIA_french
        set_hub_names = french

	}
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait = language_germanophone }
                NOT = { country_has_primary_culture = cu:french }
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_WALLONIA_german
        set_hub_names = german

    }
	else = { # switch back to defaults
		reset_state_name = yes
		reset_hub_names = yes
	}
}

STATE_GELRE_state_name_assign = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			owner = {
				any_primary_culture = {
					cu:french ?= this
				}
				NOT = { c:HRE ?= this }
			}
		}

        set_state_name = STATE_GELRE_french
        set_hub_names = french

	}
    if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
                    has_discrimination_trait = language_germanophone
                }
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_GELRE_german
        set_hub_names = german

    }
	else = { # switch back to defaults
		reset_state_name = yes
		reset_hub_names = yes
	}
}

STATE_ALSACE_LORRAINE_state_name_assign = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			owner = {
				any_primary_culture = { cu:french ?= this }
				NOT = { any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait = language_germanophone } }
			}
		}

        set_state_name = STATE_ALSACE_LORRAINE_french

	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			owner ?= {
				any_primary_culture = { has_discrimination_trait = language_germanophone }
				NOT = { any_primary_culture = { cu:french ?= this } }
			}
		}

        set_state_name = STATE_ALSACE_LORRAINE_german

        set_hub_name = {
            type = city
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_ALSACE_LORRAINE_city_german
        }
        set_hub_name = {
            type = mine
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_ALSACE_LORRAINE_mine_german
        }
	}
	else = { # switch back to defaults
		reset_state_name = yes
		reset_hub_names = yes
	}
}

STATE_BOHEMIA_state_name_assign = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			owner = {
				any_primary_culture = {
					cu:french ?= this
				}
				NOT = { c:HRE ?= this }
			}
		}

        set_state_name = STATE_BOHEMIA_french
        set_hub_names = french

	}
    else_if = { # German
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
                    has_discrimination_trait = language_germanophone
                }
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_BOHEMIA_german

        set_hub_name = {
            type = city
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_BOHEMIA_city_german
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = farm
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_BOHEMIA_farm_german
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = wood
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_BOHEMIA_wood_german
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = mine
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_BOHEMIA_mine_german
        }
    }
    else_if = { # Czech
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
                    cu:czech ?= this
                }
            }
        }

        set_state_name = STATE_BOHEMIA_czech

        set_hub_name = {
            type = city
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_BOHEMIA_city_czech
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = farm
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_BOHEMIA_farm_czech
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = wood
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_BOHEMIA_wood_czech
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = mine
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_BOHEMIA_mine_czech
        }
    }
	else = { # switch back to defaults
		reset_state_name = yes
		reset_hub_names = yes
	}
}

STATE_WEST_SWITZERLAND_state_name_assign = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			owner = {
				any_primary_culture = {
					cu:french ?= this
				}
				NOR = {
					c:HRE ?= this
					c:PRC ?= this
					AND = {
						c:FRA ?= this
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_council_republic
					}
				}
			}
		}

        set_state_name = STATE_WEST_SWITZERLAND_french
		set_hub_names = french
	}
	else_if = {	# different state name if France is Revolutionary
		limit = {
			owner = {
				OR = {
					c:PRC ?= this
					AND = {
						c:FRA ?= this
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_council_republic
					}
				}
			}
		}

        set_state_name = STATE_WEST_SWITZERLAND_sister_republic
		set_hub_names = french
	}
	else = { # switch back to defaults
		reset_state_name = yes
		reset_hub_names = yes
	}
}

	if = {
		limit = {
			owner = {

				NOR = {

	else = { # switch back to defaults
		reset_state_name = yes
		reset_hub_names = yes
	}
}

### Iberia Region

STATE_CATALONIA_state_name_assign = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
                    has_discrimination_trait = language_francophone
                }
            }
        }
        set_state_name = STATE_CATALONIA_french
        set_hub_names = french
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                any_primary_culture = {
                    cu:catalan ?= this
                }
            }
        }
        set_state_name = STATE_CATALONIA_catalan
        set_hub_names = catalan

    else_if = {

                    has_discrimination_trait = language_esperanto
                }
            }
        }
        set_state_name = STATE_CANARY_ISLANDS_esperanto
        set_hub_names = esperanto
    }
    else = {
        reset_state_name = yes
        reset_hub_names = yes
    }
}
