STATE_ULSTER_state_name_assign = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			owner = {
				c:GBR ?= this
			}
		}

        set_hub_name = {
            type = port
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_ULSTER_port_uk # GBR Ulster port is called Londonderry. In all other cases it is Derry.
        }
	}
	else = { # switch back to defaults
		reset_state_name = yes
		reset_hub_names = yes
	}
}

STATE_INGRIA_state_name_assign = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_variable = lenin_spawn

			owner = {
				country_has_primary_culture = cu:russian
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_council_republic
			}
		}

        set_hub_name = {
            type = city
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_INGRIA_city_russian_sov # St. Petersburg only becomes Leningrad if Lenin has been around to be a namesake
        }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			owner = {
				country_has_primary_culture = cu:russian
				has_technology_researched = nationalism
				NOT = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_council_republic }

				OR = {
					any_rival_country = {
						any_primary_culture = {
							has_discrimination_trait = language_germanophone
						}
					}
					any_rivaling_country = {
						any_primary_culture = {
							has_discrimination_trait = language_germanophone
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

        set_hub_name = {
            type = city
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_INGRIA_city_russian_imp # St. Petersburg only becomes Petrograd when Germanophobia becomes a thing
        }
	}
	else = { # switch back to defaults
		reset_state_name = yes
		reset_hub_names = yes
	}
}

STATE_NORTHERN_NORWAY_state_name_assign = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                c:NOR ?= this
                has_technology_researched = political_agitation # just want to gate this to Era 4
                is_subject = no
            }
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = city
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_NORTHERN_NORWAY_city_norway_modern
        }
        set_hub_name = {
            type = farm
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_NORTHERN_NORWAY_farm_norway
        }
        set_hub_name = {
            type = wood
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_NORTHERN_NORWAY_wood_norway
        }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            owner = {
                c:NOR ?= this
                has_technology_researched = political_agitation # just want to gate this to Era 4
                is_subject = yes
            }
        }

        set_hub_name = {
            type = farm
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_NORTHERN_NORWAY_farm_norway
        }
        set_hub_name = {
            type = wood
            name = HUB_NAME_STATE_NORTHERN_NORWAY_wood_norway
        }
    }
    else = { # switch back to defaults
        reset_state_name = yes
        reset_hub_names = yes
    }
}

﻿set_as_expedition_leader = {
	set_variable = expedition_leader_var
	set_character_busy_and_immortal = yes
	ROOT = {
		set_variable = { name = expedition_leader_storage_var value = prev }
	}
}

niger_river_expedition_location = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			je:je_niger_river_expedition ?= {
				"scripted_bar_progress(expedition_progress_bar)" = 1
			}
		}
		s:STATE_NIGER_DELTA = {
			random_scope_state = {
				update_expedition_location_var = yes
				save_scope_as = current_expedition_location
			}
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			je:je_niger_river_expedition ?= {
				"scripted_bar_progress(expedition_progress_bar)" = 2
			}
		}
		s:STATE_BENIN = {
			random_scope_state = {
				update_expedition_location_var = yes
				save_scope_as = current_expedition_location
			}
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			je:je_niger_river_expedition ?= {
				"scripted_bar_progress(expedition_progress_bar)" = 3
			}
		}
		s:STATE_HAUSALAND = {
			random_scope_state = {
				update_expedition_location_var = yes
				save_scope_as = current_expedition_location
			}
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			je:je_niger_river_expedition ?= {
				"scripted_bar_progress(expedition_progress_bar)" = 4
			}
		}
		s:STATE_OUTER_HAUSALAND = {
			random_scope_state = {
				update_expedition_location_var = yes
				save_scope_as = current_expedition_location
			}
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			je:je_niger_river_expedition ?= {
				"scripted_bar_progress(expedition_progress_bar)" = 5
			}
		}
		s:STATE_TIMBUKTU = {
			random_scope_state = {
				update_expedition_location_var = yes
				save_scope_as = current_expedition_location
			}
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			je:je_niger_river_expedition ?= {
				"scripted_bar_progress(expedition_progress_bar)" = 6
			}
		}
		s:STATE_EASTERN_MALI = {
			random_scope_state = {
				update_expedition_location_var = yes
				save_scope_as = current_expedition_location
			}
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			je:je_niger_river_expedition ?= {
				"scripted_bar_progress(expedition_progress_bar)" = 7
			}
		}
		s:STATE_WESTERN_MALI = {
			random_scope_state = {
				update_expedition_location_var = yes
				save_scope_as = current_expedition_location
			}
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			je:je_niger_river_expedition ?= {
				"scripted_bar_progress(expedition_progress_bar)" >= 8
			}
		}
		s:STATE_GUINEA = {
			random_scope_state = {
				update_expedition_location_var = yes
				save_scope_as = current_expedition_location
			}
		}
	}

	else = {
		s:STATE_NIGER_DELTA = {
			random_scope_state = {
				update_expedition_location_var = yes
				save_scope_as = current_expedition_location
			}
		}
	}
}

west_america_expedition_location = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			je:je_west_america_expedition ?= {
				"scripted_bar_progress(expedition_progress_bar)" < 2
			}
		}
		s:STATE_COLORADO = {
			random_scope_state = {
				update_expedition_location_var = yes
				save_scope_as = current_expedition_location
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			je:je_west_america_expedition ?= {
				"scripted_bar_progress(expedition_progress_bar)" >= 2
				"scripted_bar_progress(expedition_progress_bar)" <= 4
			}
		}
		s:STATE_UTAH = {
			random_scope_state = {
				update_expedition_location_var = yes
				save_scope_as = current_expedition_location
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			je:je_west_america_expedition ?= {
				"scripted_bar_progress(expedition_progress_bar)" > 5
				"scripted_bar_progress(expedition_progress_bar)" <= 7
			}
		}
		s:STATE_IDAHO = {
			random_scope_state = {
				update_expedition_location_var = yes
				save_scope_as = current_expedition_location
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			je:je_west_america_expedition ?= {
				"scripted_bar_progress(expedition_progress_bar)" >= 8
			}
		}
		s:STATE_OREGON = {
			random_scope_state = {
				update_expedition_location_var = yes
				save_scope_as = current_expedition_location
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		s:STATE_UTAH = {
			random_scope_state = {
				update_expedition_location_var = yes
				save_scope_as = current_expedition_location
			}
		}
	}
}

# Progress and Peril Effects
add_small_peril_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_journal_entry = je_antarctica
		}
		je:je_antarctica ?= {
			add_progress = { value = 1 name = expedition_peril_progress_bar }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_journal_entry = je_central_africa_expedition
		}
		je:je_central_africa_expedition ?= {
			add_progress = { value = 1 name = expedition_peril_progress_bar }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_journal_entry = je_congo_expedition
		}
		je:je_congo_expedition ?= {
			add_progress = { value = 1 name = expedition_peril_progress_bar }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_journal_entry = je_niger_river_expedition
		}
		je:je_niger_river_expedition ?= {
			add_progress = { value = 1 name = expedition_peril_progress_bar }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_journal_entry = je_west_america_expedition
		}
		je:je_west_america_expedition ?= {
			add_progress = { value = 1 name = expedition_peril_progress_bar }
		}
	}
	custom_tooltip = expedition_minor_peril_tt
}
add_medium_peril_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_journal_entry = je_antarctica
		}
		je:je_antarctica ?= {

update_expedition_location_var = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				ROOT.var:current_expedition_location_var ?= {
					NOT = {
						state_region = prev.state_region
					}
				}
				NOT = {
					exists = var:current_expedition_location_var
				}
			}
		}
		ROOT = {
			set_variable = { name = current_expedition_location_var value = prev }
		}
	}
}
﻿change_to_parliamentary_elective = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			government_transfer_of_power = hereditary
		}
		heir ?= {
			disinherit_character = yes
		}
	}
}

post_change_to_parliamentary_elective = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_variable = keep_current_ruler_var
			}
		}
		get_ruler_for:parliamentary_elective = {
			set_character_as_ruler = yes
		}
	}
}

change_to_presidential_elective = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			government_transfer_of_power = hereditary
		}
		heir ?= {
			disinherit_character = yes
		}
	}
}

post_change_to_presidential_elective = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_variable = keep_current_ruler_var
			}
		}
		get_ruler_for:presidential_elective = {
			set_character_as_ruler = yes
		}
	}
}

change_to_dictatorial = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			government_transfer_of_power = hereditary
		}
		heir ?= {
			disinherit_character = yes
		}
	}
}

post_change_to_dictatorial = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			c:BIC ?= this
		}
		ig:ig_industrialists ?= {
			leader = {
				set_character_as_ruler = yes
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_variable = keep_current_ruler_var
				}
			}
			get_ruler_for:dictatorial = {
				set_character_as_ruler = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

change_to_hereditary = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			government_transfer_of_power = parliamentary_elective
		}
		create_character = {
			noble =  yes
			save_scope_as = ruler_to_be
		}
	}
}

post_change_to_hereditary= {
	if = {
		limit = {
			government_transfer_of_power = parliamentary_elective
		}
		scope:ruler_to_be = {
			set_character_as_ruler = yes
		}
	}
}
﻿# Krakatoa list
krakatoa_tsunami_effect = {
	sr:region_indonesia = {
		tsunami_effect = { value = krakatoa_large_devastation }
	}
	sr:region_indochina = {
		tsunami_effect = { value = krakatoa_large_devastation }
	}
	sr:region_south_china = {
		tsunami_effect = { value = krakatoa_large_devastation }
	}
	sr:region_northeast_asia = {
		tsunami_effect = { value = krakatoa_medium_devastation }
	}
	sr:region_oceania = {
		tsunami_effect = { value = krakatoa_medium_devastation }
	}
	sr:region_east_africa = {
		tsunami_effect = { value = krakatoa_medium_devastation }
	}
	sr:region_southern_africa = {
		tsunami_effect = { value = krakatoa_medium_devastation }
	}
	sr:region_south_india = {
		tsunami_effect = { value = krakatoa_medium_devastation }
	}
	sr:region_north_india = {
		tsunami_effect = { value = krakatoa_medium_devastation }
	}
}

# Reusable Tsunami
# scope = strategic region
tsunami_effect = {
	custom_description = {
		text = "tsunami_effect"
		subject = this
		value = $value$


		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				is_coastal = yes
				is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_krakatoa_tsunami_zone
			}
			state_region = {
				add_to_temporary_list = tsunami_list
			}
		}
		every_in_list = {
			list = tsunami_list

			set_variable = { # We want Tsunamis to ignore and reset the natural disaster cooldown
				name = natural_disaster_cooldown
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
			set_variable = {
				name = is_tsunami_state
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
			remove_from_list = tsunami_list
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_harvest_condition = tsunami
					}
				}
				start_harvest_condition = tsunami
			}
			add_devastation = $value$
		}
	}
}
﻿initialize_lobby_custom_name = {

	remove_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_1
	remove_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_2
	remove_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_3
	remove_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_4
	remove_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_5
	remove_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_6
	remove_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_7
	remove_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_8
	remove_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_9
	remove_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_10
	remove_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_11
	remove_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_12
	remove_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_13
	remove_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_14
	remove_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_15
	remove_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_16
	remove_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_17
	remove_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_18
	remove_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_19
	remove_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_20

	if = {
		limit = { is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country }
		random_list = { # Custom Lobby Name
			1 = { set_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_1 } # Union
			2 = { set_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_2 } # Association
			2 = { set_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_3 } # Society
			1 = { set_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_4 } # Friendship Committee
			2 = { set_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_5 } # Fellowship
			1 = { set_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_6 } # League
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_political_lobby_type = lobby_anti_country }
		random_list = { # Custom Lobby Name
			1 = { set_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_2 } # Association
			1 = { set_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_3 } # Society
			2 = { set_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_6 } # League
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_overlord }
		random_list = { # Custom Lobby Name
			1 = { set_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_15 } # Empire Union
			2 = { set_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_16 } # Loyalist Association
			2 = { set_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_17 } # Covenant
			2 = { set_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_18 } # Union Party
			2 = { set_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_19 } # Loyal Order
			1 = { set_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_20 } # League of Loyalists
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_political_lobby_type = lobby_anti_overlord }
		random_list = { # Custom Lobby Name
			1 = { set_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_7 } # League
			2 = { set_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_8 } # National Congress
			2 = { set_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_9 } # National Union
			1 = { set_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_10 } # National Council
			2 = { set_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_11 } # National League
			1 = { set_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_12 } # Brotherhood
			2 = { set_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_13 } # National Committee
			1 = { set_variable = lobby_custom_name_var_14 } # United League
		}
	}
}

add_lobby_appeasement_from_diplomacy_bidirectional = {
	$FIRST$ = {
		every_political_lobby = {
			limit = {
				is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
				target = $SECOND$
			}
			change_appeasement = {
				amount = $PRO_AMOUNT$
				factor = $FACTOR$
			}
		}
		every_political_lobby = {
			limit = {
				is_political_lobby_type = lobby_anti_country
				target = $SECOND$
			}
			change_appeasement = {
				amount = $ANTI_AMOUNT$
				factor = $FACTOR$
			}
		}
	}

	$SECOND$ = {
		every_political_lobby = {
			limit = {
				is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_country
				target = $FIRST$
			}
			change_appeasement = {
				amount = $PRO_AMOUNT$
				factor = $FACTOR$
			}
		}
		every_political_lobby = {
			limit = {
				is_political_lobby_type = lobby_anti_country
				target = $FIRST$
			}
			change_appeasement = {
				amount = $ANTI_AMOUNT$
				factor = $FACTOR$
			}
		}
	}
}

			limit = {
				is_political_lobby_type = lobby_pro_overlord
				target = $SECOND$
			}
			change_appeasement = {

				factor = $FACTOR$
			}
		}
		every_political_lobby = {
			limit = {
				is_political_lobby_type = lobby_anti_overlord
				target = $SECOND$
			}
			change_appeasement = {
				amount = $ANTI_AMOUNT$
				factor = $FACTOR$
			}
		}
	}
}
﻿### IP3 Monuments ###

# Kaiserforum

destroy_building_kaiserforum_1 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_scope_state = {
				AND = {
					has_building = building_kaiserforum_1
					any_scope_building = {
						is_building_type = building_kaiserforum_2
						is_under_construction = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				AND = {
					has_building = building_kaiserforum_1
					any_scope_building = {
						is_building_type = building_kaiserforum_2
						is_under_construction = yes
					}
				}

			}
			remove_building = building_kaiserforum_1
		}
	}
}

destroy_building_kaiserforum_2 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_scope_state = {
				AND = {
					has_building = building_kaiserforum_2
					any_scope_building = {
						OR = {
							is_building_type = building_kaiserforum_3
							is_building_type = building_kaiserforum_4
						}
						is_under_construction = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				AND = {
					has_building = building_kaiserforum_2
					any_scope_building = {
						OR = {
							is_building_type = building_kaiserforum_3
							is_building_type = building_kaiserforum_4
						}
						is_under_construction = yes
					}
				}

			}
			remove_building = building_kaiserforum_2
		}
	}
}

﻿
set_radical_leader = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = $RAD_IG$
			$RAD_IG$ = { is_interest_group_type = ig_rural_folk }
		}
		create_character = {
			ig_leader = yes
			ideology = ideology_radical
			interest_group = ig_rural_folk
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			exists = $RAD_IG$
			$RAD_IG$ = { is_interest_group_type = ig_intelligentsia }
		}
		create_character = {
			ig_leader = yes
			ideology = ideology_radical
			interest_group = ig_intelligentsia
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			exists = $RAD_IG$
			$RAD_IG$ = { is_interest_group_type = ig_trade_unions }
		}
		create_character = {
			ig_leader = yes
			ideology = ideology_radical
			interest_group = ig_trade_unions
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			exists = $RAD_IG$
			$RAD_IG$ = { is_interest_group_type = ig_petty_bourgeoisie }
		}
		create_character = {
			ig_leader = yes
			ideology = ideology_radical
			interest_group = ig_petty_bourgeoisie
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			exists = $RAD_IG$
			$RAD_IG$ = { is_interest_group_type = ig_industrialists }
		}
		create_character = {
			ig_leader = yes
			ideology = ideology_radical
			interest_group = ig_industrialists
		}
	}
}

#Rufus is gone, so Chris is taking over the black magic file
set_closest_great_power = {
	ordered_diplomatically_relevant_country = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { THIS = ROOT }
			country_rank = rank_value:great_power
		}
		order_by = relations:root
		position = 0
		check_range_bounds = no
		save_scope_as = closest_great_power
	}
}
﻿#	Example:
#
#	example_effect = {
#		x = 100
#		y = 50
#	}
#
#
#	In a script file:
#
#	effect = {
#		example_effect = yes
#	}

resign_and_retire = {
	remove_character_role = politician
	remove_character_role = ruler
	on_remove_ruler_effects = yes
	retire_character = yes
}

relations_hit_with_power_bloc_leaders = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_game_started = yes
		}

		every_power_bloc = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { scope:power_bloc ?= this }
			}
			power_bloc_leader = {
				root = {
					change_relations = {
						country = prev
						value = {
							add = -30
							if = {
								limit = {
									is_country_type = unrecognized
									prev = { is_country_type = recognized }
								}
								add = -30
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

save_neighbor_with_state = {
	random_neighbouring_state = {
		limit = {
			owner = {
				$CRITERIA$
			}
		}
		save_scope_as = neighbor_state
		owner = {
			save_scope_as = neighbor_country
		}

		random_neighbouring_state = {
			limit = {
				owner = ROOT
			}
			save_scope_as = neighbor_neighbor_state
		}
	}
}

save_industrialized_pm_building_and_state = {
	random_scope_building = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					is_building_type = building_textile_mill
					state = {
						OR = {
							is_production_method_active = {
								building_type = building_textile_mill
								production_method = pm_mechanized_looms
							}
							is_production_method_active = {
								building_type = building_textile_mill
								production_method = pm_automatic_power_looms
							}
						}
					}
				}
				AND = {
					is_building_type = building_furniture_manufactory
					state = {
						OR = {
							is_production_method_active = {
								building_type = building_furniture_manufactory
								production_method = pm_mechanized_workshops
							}
							is_production_method_active = {
								building_type = building_furniture_manufactory
								production_method = pm_watertube_boiler_building_furniture_manufactory
							}
							is_production_method_active = {
								building_type = building_furniture_manufactory
								production_method = pm_rotary_valve_engine_building_furniture_manufactory
							}
							is_production_method_active = {
								building_type = building_furniture_manufactory
								production_method = pm_assembly_lines_building_furniture_manufactory
							}
						}
					}
				}
				AND = {
					is_building_type = building_tooling_workshop
					state = {
						OR = {
							is_production_method_active = {
								building_type = building_tooling_workshop
								production_method = pm_watertube_boiler_building_tooling_workshop
							}
							is_production_method_active = {
								building_type = building_tooling_workshop
								production_method = pm_rotary_valve_engine_building_tooling_workshop
							}
							is_production_method_active = {
								building_type = building_tooling_workshop
								production_method = pm_assembly_lines_building_tooling_workshop
							}
						}
					}
				}
				AND = {
					is_building_type = building_paper_mill
					state = {
						OR = {
							is_production_method_active = {
								building_type = building_paper_mill
								production_method = pm_watertube_boiler_building_paper_mill
							}
							is_production_method_active = {
								building_type = building_paper_mill
								production_method = pm_rotary_valve_engine_building_paper_mill
							}
						}
					}
				}
				AND = {
					is_building_type = building_steel_mill
					state = {
						OR = {
							is_production_method_active = {
								building_type = building_steel_mill
								production_method = pm_watertube_boiler_building_steel_mill
							}
							is_production_method_active = {
								building_type = building_steel_mill
								production_method = pm_rotary_valve_engine_building_steel_mill
							}
						}
					}
				}
				AND = {
					is_building_type = building_motor_industry
					state = {
						OR = {
							is_production_method_active = {
								building_type = building_motor_industry
								production_method = pm_watertube_boiler_building_motor_industry
							}
							is_production_method_active = {
								building_type = building_motor_industry
								production_method = pm_rotary_valve_engine_building_motor_industry
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		save_scope_as = industrialized_pm_building
		state = {
			save_scope_as = industrialized_pm_state
		}
	}
}

warlord_setup_effect = {
	activate_law = law_type:law_autocracy
	activate_law = law_type:law_presidential_republic
	activate_law = law_type:law_peasant_levies
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOR = {
				c:MGL ?= this
				c:TUV ?= this
				c:ALI ?= this
			}
		}
		save_scope_as = transfer_scope
		c:CHI ?= {
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_character = {
						OR = {
							has_role_of_type = general
							AND = {
								PREV = {
									any_scope_state = {
										has_building = building_naval_administration
									}
								}
								has_role_of_type = admiral
							}
						}
					}
				}
				random_scope_character = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							has_role_of_type = general
							AND = {
								PREV = {
									any_scope_state = {
										has_building = building_naval_administration
									}
								}
								has_role_of_type = admiral
							}
						}
					}
					transfer_character = scope:transfer_scope
					set_character_as_ruler = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOR = {
					country_has_primary_culture = cu:han
					country_has_primary_culture = cu:manchu
				}
			}
			add_primary_culture = cu:han
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_ethnostate
						has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_national_supremacy
					}
				}
				remove_primary_culture = cu:uighur
				remove_primary_culture = cu:tibetan
				remove_primary_culture = cu:mongol
			}
		}
	}

	every_scope_character = {
		random_list = {
			80 =  {
			}

			20 = {
				add_trait = bandit
			}
		}
	}
}

scripted_effect_parties_emergence = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_has_voting_franchise = no
		}
		custom_tooltip = PARTIES_EMERGENCE
	}

	# Will immediately trigger elections and thus update parties so no need to call update_party_support here
}

scripted_effect_parties_disappearence = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_has_voting_franchise = yes
		}
		custom_tooltip = PARTIES_DISAPPEARENCE
	}

	deactivate_parties = yes
}

enslave_discriminated_farm_workers = {
	every_scope_pop = {
		limit = {
			is_pop_type = laborers
			pop_has_primary_culture = no
			pop_acceptance < acceptance_status_4
			OR = {
				pop_employment_building_group = bg_agriculture
				pop_employment_building_group = bg_ranching
				pop_employment_building_group = bg_plantations
			}
		}
		change_poptype = pop_type:slaves
	}
}

civil_war_save_scopes = {
	save_scope_as = ongoing_revolution
	political_movement = {
		save_scope_as = ongoing_revolution_movement
	}
}

save_agitator_law_scopes = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			agitator_supports_enaction = yes
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_role_of_type = agitator
				OR = {
					law_enactment_stance = {
						law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type
						value > neutral
					}
					AND = {
						law_enactment_stance = {
							law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type
							value = neutral
						}
						interest_group ?= {
							law_enactment_stance = {
								law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type
								value > neutral
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = supporting_agitator
			interest_group ?= {
				save_scope_as = agitator_ig
			}
		}
		random_political_movement = {
			limit = {
				law_enactment_stance = {
					law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type
					value > neutral
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = supporting_movement
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			agitator_opposes_enaction = yes
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_role_of_type = agitator
				OR = {
					law_enactment_stance = {
						law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type
						value < neutral
					}
					AND = {
						law_enactment_stance = {
							law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type
							value = neutral
						}
						interest_group ?= {
							law_enactment_stance = {
								law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type
								value < neutral
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = opposing_agitator
			interest_group ?= {
				save_scope_as = agitator_ig
			}
		}
		random_political_movement = {
			limit = {
				law_enactment_stance = {
					law = owner.currently_enacting_law.type
					value < neutral
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = opposing_movement
		}
	}
}

generate_french_monarch = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_character = {
					is_heir_of_own_country = yes
					is_historical = no
				}
			}
			random_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					is_heir_of_own_country = yes
					is_historical = no
				}
				retire_character = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_gov_regency = yes
			}
			remove_regency_modifier = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = orleanist_restoration_var
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				year < 1850
			}
			country_summon_character_effect = {
				TEMPLATE = FRA_ferdinand_philippe
				ON_CREATED_EFFECT = summon_generic_make_ruler_effect
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					year > 1838
					year < 1850
				}
				country_summon_heir_effect = {
					TEMPLATE = FRA_philippe_de_orleans_of_paris
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				year >= 1850
				year < 1894
			}
			country_summon_character_effect = {
				TEMPLATE = FRA_philippe_de_orleans_of_paris
				ON_CREATED_EFFECT = summon_generic_make_ruler_effect
			}

				country_summon_heir_effect = {

				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {

			}

				TEMPLATE = FRA_henri_de_bourbon
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				year >= 1844
				year < 1883
			}
			country_summon_character_effect = {
				TEMPLATE = FRA_henri_de_bourbon
				ON_CREATED_EFFECT = summon_generic_make_ruler_effect
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				year >= 1883
				year < 1887
			}
			country_summon_character_effect = {
				TEMPLATE = SPC_juan_iii_de_borbon_template
				ON_CREATED_EFFECT = summon_legitimist_carlist_effect
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				year >= 1887
				year < 1909
			}
			country_summon_character_effect = {
				TEMPLATE = SPC_carlos_vii_de_borbon_template
				ON_CREATED_EFFECT = summon_legitimist_carlist_effect
			}
		}

exile_character_with_role_cleanup = { # scope:character - should be used instead of exile_character = yes
	exile_character = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = this.commander_military_formation
		}
		unassign_from_formation = yes
	}
	replace_character_roles = { add = { agitator } remove_all = yes }
}

clean_up_exile_character_roles = { # scope: character
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = this.commander_military_formation
		}
		unassign_from_formation = yes
	}
	replace_character_roles = { add = { agitator } remove_all = yes }
}

remove_war_disease_modifier = { #used to remove modifiers from nursing_events.1 and je_war_nursing
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_modifier = war_wounded_disease
        }
        remove_modifier = war_wounded_disease
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_modifier = war_wounded_civilian_assistance
        }
        remove_modifier = war_wounded_civilian_assistance
    }
}

unification_claims_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_state_region = {
				any_scope_state = {
					is_homeland_of_country_cultures = ROOT
					NOT = { owner = ROOT }
					NOT = { has_claim_by = ROOT }
				}
			}
		}
		every_state_region = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_state = {
					is_homeland_of_country_cultures = ROOT
					NOT = { owner = ROOT }
					NOT = { has_claim_by = ROOT }
				}
			}
			add_claim = ROOT
		}
	}
	else = {
		add_loyalists = {
			value = 0.05
		}
	}
}

remove_subject_diplomatic_pact = {
	root.power_bloc ?= {
		add_leverage = { target = prev value = -500 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			this = {
				has_diplomatic_pact = {
					who = root
					type = protectorate
				}
			}
		}
		this = {
			remove_diplomatic_pact = {
				country = root
				type = protectorate
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			this = {
				has_diplomatic_pact = {
					who = root
					type = puppet
				}
			}
		}
		this = {
			remove_diplomatic_pact = {
				country = root
				type = puppet
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			this = {
				has_diplomatic_pact = {
					who = root
					type = tributary
				}
			}
		}
		this = {
			remove_diplomatic_pact = {
				country = root
				type = tributary
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			this = {
				has_diplomatic_pact = {
					who = root
					type = vassal
				}
			}
		}
		this = {
			remove_diplomatic_pact = {
				country = root
				type = vassal
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			this = {
				has_diplomatic_pact = {
					who = root
					type = chartered_company
				}
			}
		}
		this = {
			remove_diplomatic_pact = {
				country = root
				type = chartered_company
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			this = {
				has_diplomatic_pact = {
					who = root
					type = dominion
				}
			}
		}
		this = {
			remove_diplomatic_pact = {
				country = root
				type = dominion
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			this = {
				has_diplomatic_pact = {
					who = root
					type = colony
				}
			}
		}
		this = {
			remove_diplomatic_pact = {
				country = root
				type = colony
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			this = {
				has_diplomatic_pact = {
					who = root
					type = personal_union
				}
			}
		}
		this = {
			remove_diplomatic_pact = {
				country = root
				type = personal_union
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			this = {
				has_diplomatic_pact = {
					who = root
					type = crown_land
				}
			}
		}
		this = {
			remove_diplomatic_pact = {
				country = root
				type = crown_land
			}
		}
	}
}

accepted_cultural_minority_selector_owner_scope_save_scope_as_minority_culture = {
	random_scope_state = {
		limit = {
			any_scope_culture = {
				OR = {
					has_homeland = prev
					culture_has_community_in_state = prev
				}
				prev = {
					OR  = {
						AND = {
							ROOT.owner = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_national_supremacy }
							state_cultural_acceptance = {
								target = prev
								value >= acceptance_status_5
							}
						}
						AND = {
							ROOT.owner = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_racial_segregation }
							state_cultural_acceptance = {
								target = prev
								value >= acceptance_status_4
							}
						}
						AND = {
							ROOT.owner = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_subjecthood }
							state_cultural_acceptance = {
								target = prev
								value >= acceptance_status_4
							}
						}
						AND = {
							ROOT.owner = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_cultural_exclusion }
							state_cultural_acceptance = {
								target = prev
								value >= acceptance_status_3
							}
						}
						AND = {
							ROOT.owner = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_multicultural }
							state_cultural_acceptance = {
								target = prev
								value >= acceptance_status_2
							}
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = { is_primary_culture_of = ROOT.owner }
			}
		}
		random_scope_culture = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_homeland = prev
					culture_has_community_in_state = prev
				}
				prev = {
					OR  = {
						AND = {
							ROOT.owner = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_national_supremacy }
							state_cultural_acceptance = {
								target = prev
								value >= acceptance_status_4
							}
						}
						AND = {
							ROOT.owner = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_racial_segregation }
							state_cultural_acceptance = {
								target = prev
								value >= acceptance_status_3
							}
						}
						AND = {
							ROOT.owner = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_cultural_exclusion }
							state_cultural_acceptance = {
								target = prev
								value >= acceptance_status_2
							}
						}
						AND = {
							ROOT.owner = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_multicultural }
							state_cultural_acceptance = {
								target = prev
								value >= acceptance_status_1
							}
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = { is_primary_culture_of = ROOT.owner }
			}
			save_scope_as = minority_culture
		}
	}
}

spoils_of_war_initiation_gre = { # If there are any states in the Balkans that are Greek homelands, and not ours, and if there is at least one country who completed the Balkan League JE besides us.
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = c:GRE
			c:GRE ?= THIS
			is_subject = no
			sr:region_balkans = {
				any_scope_state = {
					state_region = {
						is_homeland = cu:greek
					}
					owner = root
					count < 7
				}
			}
			has_variable = first_balkan_war_successful
	   	}
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_spoils_of_war
		}
	}
}

the_balkan_league_activated_modifier_cleanser = { #removes all activated modifiers when they're not logically applicable anymore.
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_modifier = balkan_wars_military_tech_boost_mod
		remove_modifier = balkan_wars_military_industry_overworked_mod
	}
}

# Used by the generic prestige-good JEs in 05_prestige_goods.txt. If the
# company currently saved to scope:relevant_company no longer exists
# (e.g. the player disbanded it), rebind scope:relevant_company to another
# qualifying company in the country and reset the progress timer. If no
# qualifying company exists, leaves the scope unbound — the JE's possible
# trigger will then fail and can_deactivate = yes sends it back to inactive.
rebind_prestige_good_company = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { exists = scope:relevant_company } }
		random_company = {
			limit = {
				can_potentially_produce_prestige_goods = $GOODS$
				is_producing_prestige_goods = no
				company_is_prosperous = yes
				NOT = { has_variable = $UNLOCK_VAR$ }
			}
			save_scope_as = relevant_company
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = $TIMER_VAR$
			value = 0
		}
	}
}

# Used from on_company_disbanded for a single generic prestige good. When the
# company being disbanded (scope:company) is the one that had unlocked $JE$'s
# good, its unlock variable is lost with it. If no other company in the country
# still holds that unlock, re-add the matching prestige-good JE so a future
# qualifying company can earn the good again. The disbanded company still exists
# and still holds the variable when this runs, so count >= 2 means another holder
# remains and the good is not actually lost. Runs in country scope.
re_add_prestige_good_je_if_lost = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:company ?= {
				has_variable = $UNLOCK_VAR$
			}
			NOT = {
				any_company = {
					count >= 2
					has_variable = $UNLOCK_VAR$
				}
			}
			NOT = {
				has_journal_entry = $JE$
			}
		}
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = $JE$
		}
	}
}

# Called once from on_company_disbanded. Re-adds any generic prestige-good JE
# whose good was solely produced by the company being disbanded, covering the
# case where the JE had already completed (and was therefore removed) before the
# company was dissolved.
re_add_disbanded_company_prestige_good_jes = {
	re_add_prestige_good_je_if_lost = { UNLOCK_VAR = prestige_good_generic_clothes_var JE = je_prestige_goods_clothes }
	re_add_prestige_good_je_if_lost = { UNLOCK_VAR = prestige_good_generic_groceries_var JE = je_prestige_goods_groceries }
	re_add_prestige_good_je_if_lost = { UNLOCK_VAR = prestige_good_generic_furniture_var JE = je_prestige_goods_furniture }
	re_add_prestige_good_je_if_lost = { UNLOCK_VAR = prestige_good_generic_fertilizer_var JE = je_prestige_goods_fertilizer }
	re_add_prestige_good_je_if_lost = { UNLOCK_VAR = prestige_good_generic_tools_var JE = je_prestige_goods_tools }
	re_add_prestige_good_je_if_lost = { UNLOCK_VAR = prestige_good_generic_fish_var JE = je_prestige_goods_fish }
	re_add_prestige_good_je_if_lost = { UNLOCK_VAR = prestige_good_generic_meat_var JE = je_prestige_goods_meat }
	re_add_prestige_good_je_if_lost = { UNLOCK_VAR = prestige_good_generic_coffee_var JE = je_prestige_goods_coffee }
	re_add_prestige_good_je_if_lost = { UNLOCK_VAR = prestige_good_generic_steel_var JE = je_prestige_goods_steel }
	re_add_prestige_good_je_if_lost = { UNLOCK_VAR = prestige_good_generic_opium_var JE = je_prestige_goods_opium }
	re_add_prestige_good_je_if_lost = { UNLOCK_VAR = prestige_good_generic_small_arms_var JE = je_prestige_goods_small_arms }
	re_add_prestige_good_je_if_lost = { UNLOCK_VAR = prestige_good_generic_artillery_var JE = je_prestige_goods_artillery }
	re_add_prestige_good_je_if_lost = { UNLOCK_VAR = prestige_good_generic_merchant_marine_var JE = je_prestige_goods_merchant_marine }
	re_add_prestige_good_je_if_lost = { UNLOCK_VAR = prestige_good_generic_grain_var JE = je_prestige_goods_grain }
	re_add_prestige_good_je_if_lost = { UNLOCK_VAR = prestige_good_generic_paper_var JE = je_prestige_goods_paper }
	re_add_prestige_good_je_if_lost = { UNLOCK_VAR = prestige_good_generic_explosives_var JE = je_prestige_goods_explosives }
}
﻿sepoy_mutiny_immediate_effects = {
	### Bengal Presidency
	# Mughal Uprising
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = c:MUG
		}
		c:MUG ?= {
			sepoy_mutiny_remove_puppet_effect = yes
			set_variable = sepoy_mutiny_revolter_var
			add_modifier = {
				name = war_for_independence
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = s:STATE_DELHI.region_state:BIC
			}
			s:STATE_DELHI.region_state:BIC = { set_state_owner = c:MUG }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					p:xE0EFA0.state.owner = c:MUG
				}
			}
			p:xE0EFA0.state = {
				set_state_owner = c:MUG
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { exists = c:MUG }
			exists = s:STATE_DELHI.region_state:BIC
			p:xE0EFA0.state.owner = root
		}
		create_country = {
			origin = root
			tag = MUG
			province = p:xE0EFA0
			on_created = {
				set_variable = sepoy_mutiny_revolter_var
				sepoy_mutiny_apply_laws_effect = yes
				add_modifier = {
					name = war_for_independence
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
				s:STATE_DELHI.region_state:BIC ?= { set_state_owner = c:MUG }
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		p:xE0EFA0.state.owner = {
			create_country = {
				origin = root
				tag = MUG
				province = p:xE0EFA0
				on_created = {
					set_variable = sepoy_mutiny_revolter_var
					sepoy_mutiny_apply_laws_effect = yes
					add_modifier = {
						name = war_for_independence
						days = normal_modifier_time
					}
				}
			}
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					state_region = s:STATE_DELHI
				}
				set_state_owner = c:MUG
			}
		}
	}
	# Bengal uprising
	if = {
		limit = {
			# Low Stability
			je:je_uneasy_raj = {
				"scripted_bar_progress(sepoy_mutiny_progress_bar_bengal)" <= uneasy_raj_stability_low_upper_bound
			}
			# Give East Bengal to Bengal
			NOT = { exists = c:BGL }
			exists = s:STATE_EAST_BENGAL.region_state:BIC
		}
		create_country = {
			origin = root
			tag = BGL
			state = s:STATE_EAST_BENGAL.region_state:BIC
			on_created = {
				sepoy_mutiny_apply_laws_effect = yes
				set_variable = sepoy_mutiny_revolter_var
				add_modifier = {
					name = war_for_independence
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
	}
	# Sikh uprising
	if = {
		limit = {
			# Low Stability
			je:je_uneasy_raj = {
				"scripted_bar_progress(sepoy_mutiny_progress_bar_bengal)" <= uneasy_raj_stability_low_upper_bound
			}
			# Give Hill Punjab to Sikhs
			NOT = { exists = c:PAN }
			exists = s:STATE_HILL_PUNJAB.region_state:BIC
		}
		set_global_variable = sepoy_mutiny_sikh_uprising_global_var
		create_country = {
			origin = root
			tag = PAN
			state = s:STATE_HILL_PUNJAB.region_state:BIC
			on_created = {
				set_variable = sepoy_mutiny_revolter_var
				sepoy_mutiny_apply_laws_effect = yes
				add_modifier = {
					name = war_for_independence
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
	}
	### Bombay Presidency
	# Maratha uprising
	if = {
		limit = {
			# Medium Stability
			je:je_uneasy_raj = {
				"scripted_bar_progress(sepoy_mutiny_progress_bar_bombay)" <= uneasy_raj_stability_medium_upper_bound
			}
			exists = c:SAT
		}
		c:SAT ?= {
			set_variable = sepoy_mutiny_revolter_var
			sepoy_mutiny_remove_puppet_effect = yes
			add_modifier = {
				name = war_for_independence
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			# Medium Stability
			je:je_uneasy_raj = {
				"scripted_bar_progress(sepoy_mutiny_progress_bar_bombay)" <= uneasy_raj_stability_medium_upper_bound
			}
			# Give Satara proper to Satara
			NOT = { exists = c:SAT }
			exists = s:STATE_BOMBAY.region_state:BIC
			p:x3E5505.state.owner = root
		}
		create_country = {
			origin = root
			tag = SAT
			province = p:x3E5505
			on_created = {
				s:STATE_BOMBAY = {
					set_owner_of_provinces = {
						country = c:SAT
						provinces = { x33E560 xE73C3C x47EE55 x4F5C91 xCC722D xE01853 x187C37 xCF3817 xD6660F }
					}
				}
				set_variable = sepoy_mutiny_revolter_var
				sepoy_mutiny_apply_laws_effect = yes
				add_modifier = {
					name = war_for_independence
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			# Medium Stability
			je:je_uneasy_raj = {
				"scripted_bar_progress(sepoy_mutiny_progress_bar_bombay)" <= uneasy_raj_stability_medium_upper_bound
			}
		}
		p:x3E5505.state.owner = {
			create_country = {
				origin = root
				tag = SAT
				province = p:x3E5505
				on_created = {
					s:STATE_BOMBAY = {
						set_owner_of_provinces = {
							country = c:SAT
							provinces = { x33E560 xE73C3C x47EE55 x4F5C91 xCC722D xE01853 x187C37 xCF3817 xD6660F }
						}
					}
					set_variable = sepoy_mutiny_revolter_var
					sepoy_mutiny_apply_laws_effect = yes
					add_modifier = {
						name = war_for_independence
						days = normal_modifier_time
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = { # Peshwa Event
		limit = {
			c:SAT ?= {
				has_variable = sepoy_mutiny_revolter_var
			}
			game_date < 1870.1.1
			game_date > 1845.1.1
		}
		c:SAT ?= {
			trigger_event = { id = sepoy_mutiny_events.1 popup = yes days = 7 }
		}
	}
	# Sindh uprising
	if = {
		limit = {
			# Low Stability
			je:je_uneasy_raj = {
				"scripted_bar_progress(sepoy_mutiny_progress_bar_bombay)" <= uneasy_raj_stability_low_upper_bound
			}
			# Give Sindh to Sindh
			NOT = { exists = c:SIN }
			exists = s:STATE_SINDH.region_state:BIC
		}
		create_country = {
			origin = root
			tag = SIN
			state = s:STATE_SINDH.region_state:BIC
			on_created = {
				set_variable = sepoy_mutiny_revolter_var
				sepoy_mutiny_apply_laws_effect = yes
				add_modifier = {
					name = war_for_independence
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
	}
	# Gujarat uprising
	if = {
		limit = {
			# Critical Stability
			je:je_uneasy_raj = {
				"scripted_bar_progress(sepoy_mutiny_progress_bar_bombay)" <= uneasy_raj_stability_critical_upper_bound
			}
			# Give Gujarat to Gujarat
			NOT = { exists = c:GUJ }
			exists = s:STATE_GUJARAT.region_state:BIC
		}
		create_country = {
			origin = root
			tag = GUJ
			state = s:STATE_GUJARAT.region_state:BIC
			on_created = {
				set_variable = sepoy_mutiny_revolter_var
				sepoy_mutiny_apply_laws_effect = yes
				add_modifier = {
					name = war_for_independence
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
	}
	### Madras Presidency
	# Kurnool uprising
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = c:KNO
		}
		c:KNO ?= {
			set_variable = sepoy_mutiny_revolter_var
			sepoy_mutiny_remove_puppet_effect = yes
			add_modifier = {
				name = war_for_independence
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			# Medium Stability
			je:je_uneasy_raj = {
				"scripted_bar_progress(sepoy_mutiny_progress_bar_madras)" <= uneasy_raj_stability_medium_upper_bound
			}
			# Give Kurnool proper to Kurnool
			NOT = { exists = c:KNO }
			exists = s:STATE_KURNOOL.region_state:BIC
			p:x84EE56.state.owner = this
		}
		create_country = {
			origin = root
			tag = KNO
			province = p:x84EE56
			on_created = {
				s:STATE_KURNOOL = {
					set_owner_of_provinces = {
						country = c:KNO
						provinces = { xF91482 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = sepoy_mutiny_revolter_var
				sepoy_mutiny_apply_laws_effect = yes
				add_modifier = {
					name = war_for_independence
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			# Medium Stability
			je:je_uneasy_raj = {
				"scripted_bar_progress(sepoy_mutiny_progress_bar_madras)" <= uneasy_raj_stability_medium_upper_bound
			}
		}
		p:x84EE56.state.owner = {
			create_country = {
				origin = root
				tag = KNO
				province = p:x84EE56
				on_created = {
					s:STATE_KURNOOL = {
						set_owner_of_provinces = {
							country = c:KNO
							provinces = { xF91482 }
						}
					}
					set_variable = sepoy_mutiny_revolter_var
					sepoy_mutiny_apply_laws_effect = yes
					add_modifier = {
						name = war_for_independence
						days = normal_modifier_time
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	# Carnatic uprising
	if = {
		limit = {
			# Critical Stability
			je:je_uneasy_raj = {
				"scripted_bar_progress(sepoy_mutiny_progress_bar_bombay)" <= uneasy_raj_stability_critical_upper_bound
			}
			# Give Madras to Carnatic
			NOT = { exists = c:CAR }
			exists = s:STATE_MADRAS.region_state:BIC
		}
		create_country = {
			origin = root
			tag = CAR
			province = p:x99B424
			on_created = {
				sepoy_mutiny_apply_laws_effect = yes
				s:STATE_MADRAS = {
					set_owner_of_provinces = {
						country = c:CAR
						provinces = { x99B424 x7CCE50 xA3158E xA63901 x5AF5BE xEC1945 x6A381C }
					}
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = war_for_independence
					days = normal_modifier_time
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

sepoy_mutiny_bengal_effect_low = {
	# Destroy a random non-capital barracks
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_scope_state = {
				OR = {
					region = sr:region_north_india
					region = sr:region_north_india
					region = sr:region_north_india
				}
				NOT = {
					state_region = s:STATE_WEST_BENGAL
				}
				any_scope_building = {
					is_building_type = building_barrack
				}
			}
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					region = sr:region_north_india
					region = sr:region_north_india
					region = sr:region_north_india
				}
				NOT = {
					state_region = s:STATE_WEST_BENGAL
				}
				any_scope_building = {
					is_building_type = building_barrack
				}
			}
			remove_building = building_barrack
			add_modifier = {
				name = sepoy_mutiny_barracks_destruction_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
				multiplier = 0.5
			}
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = small_radicals
			}
			save_scope_as = bengal_sepoy_barracks_scope
		}
	}
	# Add negative modifiers to every other barracks
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_scope_state = {
				OR = {
					region = sr:region_north_india
					region = sr:region_north_india
					region = sr:region_north_india
				}
				any_scope_building = {
					is_building_type = building_barrack
				}
				NOT = {
					scope:bengal_sepoy_barracks_scope ?= this
				}
			}
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					region = sr:region_north_india
					region = sr:region_north_india
					region = sr:region_north_india
				}
				any_scope_building = {
					is_building_type = building_barrack
				}
				NOT = {
					scope:bengal_sepoy_barracks_scope ?= this
				}
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = sepoy_mutiny_barracks_unrest_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
				multiplier = 0.5
			}
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = small_radicals
			}
		}
	}
	# Mughal uprising
	# Mughal independence war diplomatic play
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = c:MUG
			NOT = {
				any_diplomatic_play = {
					target = { c:MUG ?= this }
					initiator = { c:BIC ?= this }
					is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_secession
				}
			}
		}
		create_diplomatic_play = {
			name = sepoy_mutiny
			type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
			escalation = 80
			target_country = c:MUG
		}
		# Add Britain
		random_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
				target = {
					c:MUG ?= this
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					initiator = {
						is_subject = yes
					}
				}
				add_initiator_backers = { initiator.top_overlord }
			}
		}
		# Add wargoals
		random_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
				target = {
					c:MUG ?= this
				}
			}
			# Agra State
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = s:STATE_AGRA.region_state:BIC
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:MUG
					type = conquer_state
					primary_demand = yes
					target_state = s:STATE_AGRA.region_state:BIC
				}
			}
			# Oudh State
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = s:STATE_AWADH.region_state:BIC
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:MUG
					type = conquer_state
					primary_demand = yes
					target_state = s:STATE_AWADH.region_state:BIC
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOR = {
						exists = s:STATE_AGRA.region_state:BIC
						exists = s:STATE_AWADH.region_state:BIC
					}
					exists = c:BIC
				}
				c:BIC ?= {
					save_temporary_scope_value_as = {
						name = war_reparations_amount
						value = {
							value = fixed_income
							multiply = define:NDiplomacy|DEFAULT_WAR_REPARATIONS_MONEY_TRANSFER
						}
					}
				}

				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:MUG
					target_country = c:BIC
					type = enforce_treaty_article
					primary_demand = yes
					article = {
						article = money_transfer
						source_country = c:BIC
						target_country = c:MUG
						inputs = {
							{ quantity = scope:war_reparations_amount }
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
sepoy_mutiny_bengal_effect_medium = {
	# Destroy a random non-capital barracks
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_scope_state = {
				OR = {
					region = sr:region_north_india
					region = sr:region_north_india
					region = sr:region_north_india
				}
				NOT = {
					state_region = s:STATE_WEST_BENGAL
				}
				any_scope_building = {
					is_building_type = building_barrack
				}
			}
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					region = sr:region_north_india
					region = sr:region_north_india
					region = sr:region_north_india
				}
				NOT = {
					state_region = s:STATE_WEST_BENGAL
				}
				any_scope_building = {
					is_building_type = building_barrack
				}
			}
			remove_building = building_barrack
			add_modifier = {
				name = sepoy_mutiny_barracks_destruction_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
			}
			save_scope_as = bengal_sepoy_barracks_scope
		}
	}
	# Add negative modifiers to every other barracks
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_scope_state = {
				OR = {
					region = sr:region_north_india
					region = sr:region_north_india
					region = sr:region_north_india
				}
				any_scope_building = {
					is_building_type = building_barrack
				}
				NOT = {
					scope:bengal_sepoy_barracks_scope ?= this
				}
			}
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					region = sr:region_north_india
					region = sr:region_north_india
					region = sr:region_north_india
				}
				any_scope_building = {
					is_building_type = building_barrack
				}
				NOT = {
					scope:bengal_sepoy_barracks_scope ?= this
				}
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = sepoy_mutiny_barracks_unrest_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
			}
		}
	}
	# Mughal uprising
	# Give Agra to Mughals
	if = {
		limit = { exists = s:STATE_AWADH.region_state:BIC }
		s:STATE_AGRA.region_state:BIC = { set_state_owner = c:MUG }
	}
	# Mughal independence war diplomatic play
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = c:MUG
			NOT = {
				any_diplomatic_play = {
					target = { c:MUG ?= this }
					initiator = { c:BIC ?= this }
					is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_secession
				}
			}
		}
		create_diplomatic_play = {
			name = sepoy_mutiny
			type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
			escalation = 80
			target_country = c:MUG
		}
		# Add Britain
		random_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
				target = {
					c:MUG ?= this
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					initiator = {
						is_subject = yes
					}
				}
				add_initiator_backers = { initiator.top_overlord }
			}
		}
		# Add wargoals
		random_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
				target = {
					c:MUG ?= this
				}
			}
			# Oudh State
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = s:STATE_AWADH.region_state:BIC
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:MUG
					type = conquer_state
					primary_demand = yes
					target_state = s:STATE_AWADH.region_state:BIC
				}
			}
			else = {
				c:BIC ?= {
					save_temporary_scope_value_as = {
						name = war_reparations_amount
						value = {
							value = fixed_income
							multiply = define:NDiplomacy|DEFAULT_WAR_REPARATIONS_MONEY_TRANSFER
						}
					}
				}

				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:MUG
					target_country = c:BIC
					type = enforce_treaty_article
					primary_demand = yes
					article = {
						article = money_transfer
						source_country = c:BIC
						target_country = c:MUG
						inputs = {
							{ quantity = scope:war_reparations_amount }
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
sepoy_mutiny_bengal_effect_high = {
	# Destroy a random non-capital barracks
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_scope_state = {
				OR = {
					region = sr:region_north_india
					region = sr:region_north_india
					region = sr:region_north_india
				}
				NOT = {
					state_region = s:STATE_WEST_BENGAL
				}
				any_scope_building = {
					is_building_type = building_barrack
				}
			}
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					region = sr:region_north_india
					region = sr:region_north_india
					region = sr:region_north_india
				}
				NOT = {
					state_region = s:STATE_WEST_BENGAL
				}
				any_scope_building = {
					is_building_type = building_barrack
				}
			}
			remove_building = building_barrack
			add_modifier = {
				name = sepoy_mutiny_barracks_destruction_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
				multiplier = 1.5
			}
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = large_radicals
			}
			save_scope_as = bengal_sepoy_barracks_scope
		}
	}
	# Add negative modifiers to every other barracks
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_scope_state = {
				OR = {
					region = sr:region_north_india
					region = sr:region_north_india
					region = sr:region_north_india
				}
				any_scope_building = {
					is_building_type = building_barrack
				}
				NOT = {
					scope:bengal_sepoy_barracks_scope ?= this
				}
			}
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					region = sr:region_north_india
					region = sr:region_north_india
					region = sr:region_north_india
				}
				any_scope_building = {
					is_building_type = building_barrack
				}
				NOT = {
					scope:bengal_sepoy_barracks_scope ?= this
				}
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = sepoy_mutiny_barracks_unrest_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
				multiplier = 1.5
			}
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = large_radicals
			}
		}
	}
	# Mughal uprising
	# Give Agra to Mughals
	if = {
		limit = { exists = s:STATE_AGRA.region_state:BIC }
		s:STATE_AGRA.region_state:BIC = { set_state_owner = c:MUG }
	}
	# Give Oudh to Mughals
	if = {
		limit = { exists = s:STATE_AWADH.region_state:BIC }
		s:STATE_AWADH.region_state:BIC = { set_state_owner = c:MUG }
	}
	# Mughal independence war diplomatic play
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = c:MUG
			NOT = {
				any_diplomatic_play = {
					target = { c:MUG ?= this }
					initiator = { c:BIC ?= this }
					is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_secession
				}
			}
		}
		create_diplomatic_play = {
			name = sepoy_mutiny
			type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
			escalation = 80
			target_country = c:MUG
		}
		# Add Britain
		random_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
				target = {
					c:MUG ?= this
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					initiator = {
						is_subject = yes
					}
				}
				add_initiator_backers = { initiator.top_overlord }
			}
		}
		# Add wargoals
		random_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
				target = {
					c:MUG ?= this
				}
			}
			c:BIC ?= {
				save_temporary_scope_value_as = {
					name = war_reparations_amount
					value = {
						value = fixed_income
						multiply = define:NDiplomacy|DEFAULT_WAR_REPARATIONS_MONEY_TRANSFER
					}
				}
			}

			add_war_goal = {
				holder = c:MUG
				target_country = c:BIC
				type = enforce_treaty_article
				primary_demand = yes
				article = {
					article = money_transfer
					source_country = c:BIC
					target_country = c:MUG
					inputs = {
						{ quantity = scope:war_reparations_amount }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	# Sikh uprising
	# Sikh independence war diplomatic play
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_variable = sepoy_mutiny_sikh_uprising_global_var
		}
		random_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
				target = {
					c:MUG ?= this
				}
			}
			add_target_backers = { c:PAN }
			if = {
				limit = {
					initiator = {
						is_subject = yes
					}
				}
				add_initiator_backers = { initiator.top_overlord }
			}
		}
		# Add wargoals
		random_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
				target = {
					c:MUG ?= this
				}
			}
			# Annexing Sikhs
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:BGL
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:BIC
					type = annex_country
					primary_demand = yes
					target_country = c:PAN
				}
			}
			# Punjab State
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = s:STATE_PUNJAB.region_state:BIC
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:PAN
					type = conquer_state
					primary_demand = yes
					target_state = s:STATE_PUNJAB.region_state:BIC
				}
			}
			# North West Frontier State
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = s:STATE_PASHTUNISTAN.region_state:BIC
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:PAN
					type = conquer_state
					primary_demand = yes
					target_state = s:STATE_PASHTUNISTAN.region_state:BIC
				}
			}
		}
	}
	# Bengal independence war diplomatic play
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = c:BGL
			NOT = {
				any_diplomatic_play = {
					target = { c:BGL ?= this }
					initiator = { c:BIC ?= this }
					is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_secession
				}
			}
		}
		random_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
				target = {
					c:MUG ?= this
				}
			}
			add_target_backers = { c:BGL }
		}
		# Add wargoals
		random_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
				target = {
					c:MUG ?= this
				}
			}
			# Annexing Bengal
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:BGL
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:BIC
					type = annex_country
					primary_demand = yes
					target_country = c:BGL
				}
			}
			# Bihar State
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = s:STATE_BIHAR.region_state:BIC
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:BGL
					type = conquer_state
					primary_demand = yes
					target_state = s:STATE_BIHAR.region_state:BIC
				}
			}
			# West Bengal State
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = s:STATE_WEST_BENGAL.region_state:BIC
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:BGL
					type = conquer_state
					primary_demand = yes
					target_state = s:STATE_WEST_BENGAL.region_state:BIC
				}
			}
			# Assam State
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = s:STATE_ASSAM.region_state:BIC
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:BGL
					type = conquer_state
					primary_demand = yes
					target_state = s:STATE_ASSAM.region_state:BIC
				}
			}
			# Orissa State
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = s:STATE_ORISSA.region_state:BIC
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:BGL
					type = conquer_state
					primary_demand = yes
					target_state = s:STATE_ORISSA.region_state:BIC
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

				days = normal_modifier_time

			}
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = very_large_radicals
			}

				multiplier = 2
			}
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = very_large_radicals
			}
		}
	}

		limit = { exists = s:STATE_AGRA.region_state:BIC }
		s:STATE_AGRA.region_state:BIC = { set_state_owner = c:MUG }

	if = {
		limit = { exists = s:STATE_AWADH.region_state:BIC }
		s:STATE_AWADH.region_state:BIC = { set_state_owner = c:MUG }
	}
	# Mughal independence war diplomatic play
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = c:MUG

				any_diplomatic_play = {
					target = { c:MUG ?= this }
					initiator = { c:BIC ?= this }
					is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_secession
				}
			}
		}
		create_diplomatic_play = {
			name = sepoy_mutiny
			type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
			escalation = 80

		}

		random_diplomatic_play = {

				is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
				target = {
					c:MUG ?= this
				}
			}

			c:BIC ?= {
				save_temporary_scope_value_as = {
					name = war_reparations_amount
					value = {

						multiply = define:NDiplomacy|DEFAULT_WAR_REPARATIONS_MONEY_TRANSFER

					source_country = c:BIC
					target_country = c:MUG
					inputs = {
						{ quantity = scope:war_reparations_amount }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	# Sikh uprising
	# Give Punjab to Sikhs
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_variable = sepoy_mutiny_sikh_uprising_global_var
			exists = c:PAN
			exists = s:STATE_PUNJAB.region_state:BIC
		}
		s:STATE_PUNJAB.region_state:BIC = { set_state_owner = c:PAN }
	}
	# Sikh independence war diplomatic play
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = c:PAN
			has_global_variable = sepoy_mutiny_sikh_uprising_global_var
			NOT = {
				any_diplomatic_play = {
					target = { c:PAN ?= this }
					initiator = { c:BIC ?= this }
					is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_secession
				}
			}
		}
		random_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
				target = {
					c:MUG ?= this
				}
			}
			add_target_backers = { c:PAN }
		}
		# Add wargoals
		random_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
				target = {
					c:MUG ?= this
				}
			}
			# North West Frontier State
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = s:STATE_PASHTUNISTAN.region_state:BIC
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:PAN
					type = conquer_state
					primary_demand = yes
					target_state = s:STATE_PASHTUNISTAN.region_state:BIC
				}
			}
			# Annexing Sikhs
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:PAN
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:BIC
					type = annex_country
					primary_demand = yes
					target_country = c:PAN
				}
			}
		}
	}
	# Give Bihar to Bengal
	if = {
		limit = { exists = s:STATE_BIHAR.region_state:BIC }
		s:STATE_BIHAR.region_state:BIC = { set_state_owner = c:BGL }
	}
	# Bengal independence war diplomatic play
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = c:BGL
			NOT = {
				any_diplomatic_play = {
					target = { c:BGL ?= this }
					initiator = { c:BIC ?= this }
					is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_secession
				}
			}
		}
		random_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
				target = {
					c:MUG ?= this
				}
			}
			add_target_backers = { c:BGL }
		}
		# Add wargoals
		random_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
				target = {
					c:MUG ?= this
				}
			}
			# Bihar State
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = s:STATE_BIHAR.region_state:BIC
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:BGL
					type = conquer_state
					primary_demand = yes
					target_state = s:STATE_BIHAR.region_state:BIC
				}
			}
			# West Bengal State
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = s:STATE_WEST_BENGAL.region_state:BIC
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:BGL
					type = conquer_state
					primary_demand = yes
					target_state = s:STATE_WEST_BENGAL.region_state:BIC
				}
			}
			# Assam State
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = s:STATE_ASSAM.region_state:BIC
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:BGL
					type = conquer_state
					primary_demand = yes
					target_state = s:STATE_ASSAM.region_state:BIC
				}
			}
			# Orissa State
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = s:STATE_ORISSA.region_state:BIC
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:BGL
					type = conquer_state
					primary_demand = yes
					target_state = s:STATE_ORISSA.region_state:BIC
				}
			}
			# Annexing Bengal
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:BGL
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:BIC
					type = annex_country
					primary_demand = yes
					target_country = c:BGL
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	if = {

			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = medium_radicals
			}
		}
	}
	# Maratha independence war diplomatic play
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = c:SAT
			NOT = {
				any_diplomatic_play = {
					target = { c:SAT ?= this }
					initiator = { c:BIC ?= this }
					is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_secession
				}
			}
		}
		create_diplomatic_play = {
			name = maratha_rebellion
			type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
			escalation = 80
			target_country = c:SAT
		}
		# Add Britain
		random_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
				target = {
					c:SAT ?= this
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					initiator = {
						is_subject = yes
					}
				}
				add_initiator_backers = { initiator.top_overlord }
			}
		}
		# Add wargoals
		random_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
				target = {
					c:SAT ?= this
				}
			}
			# Bombay State
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = s:STATE_BOMBAY.region_state:BIC
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:SAT
					type = conquer_state
					primary_demand = yes
					target_state = s:STATE_BOMBAY.region_state:BIC
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
sepoy_mutiny_bombay_effect_high = {
	# High Effect
	# Destroy a random non-capital barracks
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_scope_state = {
				region = sr:region_south_india
				NOT = {
					state_region = s:STATE_BOMBAY
				}
				any_scope_building = {
					is_building_type = building_barrack
				}
			}
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				region = sr:region_south_india
				NOT = {
					state_region = s:STATE_BOMBAY
				}
				any_scope_building = {
					is_building_type = building_barrack
				}
			}
			remove_building = building_barrack
			add_modifier = {
				name = sepoy_mutiny_barracks_destruction_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
				multiplier = 1.5
			}
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = large_radicals
			}
			save_scope_as = bengal_sepoy_barracks_scope
		}
	}
	# Add negative modifiers to every other barracks
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_scope_state = {
				region = sr:region_south_india
				NOT = {
					scope:bengal_sepoy_barracks_scope ?= this
				}
			}
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				region = sr:region_south_india
				NOT = {
					scope:bengal_sepoy_barracks_scope ?= this
				}
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = sepoy_mutiny_barracks_unrest_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
				multiplier = 1.5
			}
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = large_radicals
			}
		}
	}
	# Maratha independence war diplomatic play
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = c:SAT
			NOT = {
				any_diplomatic_play = {
					target = { c:SAT ?= this  }
					initiator = { c:BIC ?= this }
					is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_secession
				}
			}
		}
		create_diplomatic_play = {
			name = maratha_rebellion
			type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
			escalation = 80
			target_country = c:SAT
		}
		# Add Britain
		random_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
				target = {
					c:SAT ?= this
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					initiator = {
						is_subject = yes
					}
				}
				add_initiator_backers = { initiator.top_overlord }
			}
		}
		# Add wargoals
		random_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
				target = {
					c:SAT ?= this
				}
			}
			# Bombay State
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = s:STATE_BOMBAY.region_state:BIC
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:SAT
					type = conquer_state
					primary_demand = yes
					target_state = s:STATE_BOMBAY.region_state:BIC
				}
			}
		}
	}
	# Sindh independence war diplomatic play
	random_diplomatic_play = {
		limit = {
			is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
			target = {
				c:SAT ?= this
			}
		}
		add_target_backers = { c:SIN }
		# Add wargoals
		# Annexing Sindh
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:SIN
			}
			add_war_goal = {
				holder = c:BIC
				type = annex_country
				primary_demand = yes
				target_country = c:SIN
			}
		}
	}
}
sepoy_mutiny_bombay_effect_critical = {
	# Critical Effect
	# Destroy a random non-capital barracks
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_scope_state = {
				region = sr:region_south_india
				NOT = {
					state_region = s:STATE_BOMBAY
				}
				any_scope_building = {
					is_building_type = building_barrack
				}
			}
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				region = sr:region_south_india
				NOT = {
					state_region = s:STATE_BOMBAY
				}
				any_scope_building = {
					is_building_type = building_barrack
				}
			}
			remove_building = building_barrack
			add_modifier = {
				name = sepoy_mutiny_barracks_destruction_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
				multiplier = 2
			}
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = very_large_radicals
			}
			save_scope_as = bengal_sepoy_barracks_scope
		}
	}
	# Add negative modifiers to every other barracks
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_scope_state = {
				region = sr:region_south_india
				NOT = {
					scope:bengal_sepoy_barracks_scope ?= this
				}
			}
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				region = sr:region_south_india
				NOT = {
					scope:bengal_sepoy_barracks_scope ?= this
				}
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = sepoy_mutiny_barracks_unrest_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
				multiplier = 2
			}
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = very_large_radicals
			}
		}
	}
	# Maratha independence war diplomatic play
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = c:SAT
			NOT = {
				any_diplomatic_play = {
					target = { c:SAT ?= this }
					initiator = { c:BIC ?= this }
					is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_secession
				}
			}
		}
		create_diplomatic_play = {
			name = maratha_rebellion
			type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
			escalation = 80
			target_country = c:SAT
		}
		# Add Britain
		random_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
				target = {
					c:SAT ?= this
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					initiator = {
						is_subject = yes
					}
				}
				add_initiator_backers = { initiator.top_overlord }
			}
		}
		# Add wargoals
		random_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
				target = {
					c:SAT ?= this
				}
			}
			# Bombay State
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = s:STATE_BOMBAY.region_state:BIC
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:SAT
					type = conquer_state
					primary_demand = yes
					target_state = s:STATE_BOMBAY.region_state:BIC
				}
			}
		}
	}
	# Sindh independence war diplomatic play
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = c:SIN
			NOT = {
				any_diplomatic_play = {
					target = { c:SIN ?= this }
					initiator = { c:BIC ?= this }
					is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_secession
				}
			}
		}
		random_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
				target = {
					c:SAT ?= this
				}
			}
			add_target_backers = { c:SIN }
			# Annexing Sindh
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:SIN
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:BIC
					type = annex_country
					primary_demand = yes
					target_country = c:SIN
				}
			}
		}
	}
	# Gujarat independence war diplomatic play
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = c:GUJ
			NOT = {
				any_diplomatic_play = {
					target = { c:GUJ ?= this }
					initiator = { c:BIC ?= this}
					is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_secession
				}
			}
		}
		random_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
				target = {
					c:SAT ?= this
				}
			}
			add_target_backers = { c:GUJ }
			# Add wargoals
			# Gujarat State
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = s:STATE_GUJARAT.region_state:BIC
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:GUJ
					type = conquer_state
					primary_demand = yes
					target_state = s:STATE_GUJARAT.region_state:BIC
				}
			}
			# Annexing Gujarat
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:GUJ
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:BIC
					type = annex_country
					primary_demand = yes
					target_country = c:GUJ
				}
			}
		}
	}

			}

					c:KNO ?= this
				}
			}

	}
	# Add negative modifiers to every other barracks
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_scope_state = {
				region = sr:region_south_india
				NOT = {
					scope:bengal_sepoy_barracks_scope ?= this
				}
			}
		}
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				region = sr:region_south_india
				NOT = {
					scope:bengal_sepoy_barracks_scope ?= this
				}
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = sepoy_mutiny_barracks_unrest_modifier
				days = normal_modifier_time
				multiplier = 2
			}
			add_radicals_in_state = {
				value = very_large_radicals
			}
		}
	}
	# Give all of Kurnool to Kurnool
	if = {
		limit = { exists = s:STATE_KURNOOL.region_state:BIC }
		s:STATE_KURNOOL.region_state:BIC = { set_state_owner = c:KNO }
	}
	# Kurnool independence war diplomatic play
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = c:KNO
			NOT = {
				any_diplomatic_play = {
					target = { c:KNO ?= this }
					initiator = { c:BIC ?= this }
					is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_secession
				}
			}
		}
		create_diplomatic_play = {
			name = kurnool_uprising
			type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
			escalation = 80
			target_country = c:KNO
		}
		# Add Britain
		random_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
				target = {
					c:KNO ?= this
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					initiator = {
						is_subject = yes
					}
				}
				add_initiator_backers = { initiator.top_overlord }
			}
		}
		# Add wargoals
		random_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
				target = {
					c:KNO ?= this
				}
			}
			# Kurnool State
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = s:STATE_KURNOOL.region_state:BIC
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:KNO
					type = conquer_state
					primary_demand = yes
					target_state = s:STATE_KURNOOL.region_state:BIC
				}
			}
			# Northern Circars State
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = s:STATE_CIRCARS.region_state:BIC
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:KNO
					type = conquer_state
					primary_demand = yes
					target_state = s:STATE_CIRCARS.region_state:BIC
				}
			}
		}
	}
	# Carnatic independence war diplomatic play
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = c:CAR
			NOT = {
				any_diplomatic_play = {
					target = { c:CAR ?= this }
					initiator = { c:BIC ?= this}
					is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_secession
				}
			}
		}
		# Add wargoals
		random_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_sepoy_mutiny
				target = {
					c:KNO ?= this
				}
			}
			add_target_backers = { c:CAR }
			# Madras State
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = s:STATE_MADRAS.region_state:BIC
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:CAR
					type = conquer_state
					primary_demand = yes
					target_state = s:STATE_MADRAS.region_state:BIC
				}
			}
			# Annexing Carnatic
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:CAR
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:BIC
					type = annex_country
					primary_demand = yes
					target_country = c:CAR
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# princely states with low liberty desire join the EIC in the war. Princely states with medium liberty desire will stay neutral (exempt from service)
sepoy_mutiny_princely_states_stance_effect = {
	every_subject_or_below = {
		limit = {
			capital = {
				OR = {
					region = sr:region_north_india
					region = sr:region_south_india
					region = sr:region_south_india
					region = sr:region_north_india
					region = sr:region_north_india
					region = sr:region_himalayas
					region = sr:region_greater_persia
				}
			}
			liberty_desire >= 75
		}
		set_variable = { name = sepoy_mutiny_princely_state_low_loyalty_var years = 5 }
		sepoy_mutiny_remove_puppet_effect = yes
	}
	every_subject_or_below = {
		limit = {
			capital = {
				OR = {
					region = sr:region_north_india
					region = sr:region_south_india
					region = sr:region_south_india
					region = sr:region_north_india
					region = sr:region_north_india
					region = sr:region_himalayas
					region = sr:region_greater_persia
				}
			}
			liberty_desire < define:NDiplomacy|WAR_GOAL_INFAMY_LOW_LIBERTY_DESIRE_THRESHOLD
			liberty_desire > 75
		}
		set_variable = sepoy_mutiny_princely_state_medium_loyalty_var
		root = {
			create_diplomatic_pact = {
				country = prev
				type = exempt_from_service
			}
		}
	}
}

sepoy_mutiny_remove_puppet_effect = {
	c:BIC.power_bloc ?= {
		add_leverage = { target = PREV value = -500 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			this = {
				has_diplomatic_pact = {
					who = c:BIC
					type = tributary
				}
			}
		}
		this = {
			remove_diplomatic_pact = {
				country = c:BIC
				type = tributary
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			this = {
				has_diplomatic_pact = {
					who = c:BIC
					type = vassal
				}
			}
		}
		this = {
			remove_diplomatic_pact = {
				country = c:BIC
				type = vassal
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			this = {
				has_diplomatic_pact = {
					who = c:BIC
					type = protectorate
				}
			}
		}
		this = {
			remove_diplomatic_pact = {
				country = c:BIC
				type = protectorate
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			this = {
				has_diplomatic_pact = {
					who = c:BIC
					type = puppet
				}
			}
		}
		this = {
			remove_diplomatic_pact = {
				country = c:BIC
				type = puppet
			}
		}
	}
}

sepoy_mutiny_apply_laws_effect = {
	set_country_type = unrecognized
	activate_law = law_type:law_monarchy
	activate_law = law_type:law_autocracy
}
