﻿country_restore_japanese_emperor = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_scope_character = {
				has_trait = special_character_japanese_emperor
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_gov_regency = yes
			}
			heir ?= {
				remove_character_role = heir # Child-Shoguns get no shot
			}
			remove_regency_modifier = yes
		}
		else = {
			heir ?= {
				retire_character = yes
			}
			ruler ?= {
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							has_role = character_role_general
							has_role = character_role_admiral
						}
					}
					set_commander_rank = 3
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_role = character_role_politician
						}
					}
					add_character_role = character_role_politician
				}
				set_variable = former_shogun_var # designates Taikun
				set_as_interest_group_leader = yes # Landowners leader, typically
				hidden_effect = { set_character_immortal = yes }
				set_variable = { name = temporary_immortality_var value = 36 }
			}
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_trait = special_character_japanese_emperor
			}
			set_character_as_ruler = yes
		}
		japan_generate_scripted_heirs = yes
	}
	else = { # Should never happen, but just in case
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_gov_regency = yes
			}
			heir ?= {
				remove_character_role = heir # Child-Shoguns get no shot
			}
			remove_regency_modifier = yes
		}
		else = {
			heir ?= {
				retire_character = yes
			}
			ruler ?= {
				set_variable = former_shogun_var # designates Taikun
				set_as_interest_group_leader = yes # Landowners leader, typically
				set_character_immortal = yes
				set_variable = { name = temporary_immortality_var value = 36 }
			}
		}
		country_generate_japanese_emperor = yes
	}
}

country_generate_japanese_emperor = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			year < 1846
		}
		create_character = {
			template = JAP_ninko_yamato
			on_created = {
				set_character_as_ruler = yes
			}
		}
		create_character = {
			template = JAP_komei_yamato
			on_created = {
				replace_character_roles = { add = { heir } remove_all = yes }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			year >= 1846
			year < 1867
		}
		create_character = {
			template = JAP_komei_yamato
			on_created = {
				set_character_as_ruler = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				year >= 1852
			}
			create_character = {
				template = JAP_meiji_yamato
				on_created = {
					replace_character_roles = { add = { heir } remove_all = yes }
				}
			}
			set_variable = heir_meiji_spawned
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			year >= 1867
			year < 1912
		}
		create_character = {
			template = JAP_meiji_yamato
			on_created = {
				set_character_as_ruler = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				year >= 1879
			}
			create_character = {
				template = JAP_taisho_yamato
				on_created = {
					replace_character_roles = { add = { heir } remove_all = yes }
				}
			}
			set_variable = heir_taisho_spawned
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			year >= 1912
			year < 1927
		}
		create_character = {
			template = JAP_taisho_yamato
			on_created = {
				set_character_as_ruler = yes
			}
		}
		create_character = {
			template = JAP_showa_yamato
			on_created = {
				replace_character_roles = { add = { heir } remove_all = yes }
			}
		}
		set_variable = heir_showa_spawned
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			year >= 1927
			year < 1989
		}
		create_character = {
			template = JAP_showa_yamato
			on_created = {
				set_character_as_ruler = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

country_generate_japanese_shogun = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			year < 1841
		}
		create_character = {
			template = JAP_ienari_tokugawa
		}
		create_character = {
			template = JAP_ieyoshi_tokugawa
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			year >= 1841
			year < 1853
		}
		create_character = {
			template = JAP_ieyoshi_tokugawa
			on_created = {
				set_character_as_ruler = yes
			}
		}
		create_character = {
			template = JAP_iesada_tokugawa
			on_created = {
				add_character_role = heir
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			year >= 1853
			year < 1859
		}
		create_character = {
			template = JAP_iesada_tokugawa
			on_created = {
				set_character_as_ruler = yes
			}
		}
		create_character = {
			template = JAP_iemochi_tokugawa
			on_created = {
				add_character_role = heir
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			year >= 1859
			year < 1867
		}
		create_character = {
			template = JAP_iemochi_tokugawa
			on_created = {
				set_character_as_ruler = yes
			}
		}
		create_character = {
			template = JAP_yoshinobu_tokugawa
			on_created = {
				add_character_role = heir
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			year >= 1867
			year < 1879
		}
		create_character = {
			template = JAP_yoshinobu_tokugawa
			on_created = {
				set_character_as_ruler = yes
			}
		}
		create_character = {
			template = JAP_iesato_tokugawa
			on_created = {
				add_character_role = heir
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			year >= 1879
			year < 1940
		}
		create_character = {
			template = JAP_iesato_tokugawa
			on_created = {
				set_character_as_ruler = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

country_ezo_generate_next_japanese_shogun = { # Used in the Ezo content - 
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_template_used = JAP_ienari_tokugawa
			NOT = {
				is_template_used = JAP_ieyoshi_tokugawa
			}
			game_date >= 1793.6.22
		}
		create_character = {
			template = JAP_ieyoshi_tokugawa
			on_created = {
				set_character_as_ruler = yes
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			is_template_used = JAP_ieyoshi_tokugawa
			NOT = {
				is_template_used = JAP_iesada_tokugawa
			}
			game_date >= 1824.5.6
		}
		create_character = {
			template = JAP_iesada_tokugawa
			on_created = {
				set_character_as_ruler = yes
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			is_template_used = JAP_iesada_tokugawa
			NOT = {
				is_template_used = JAP_iemochi_tokugawa
			}
			game_date >= 1846.7.16
		}
		create_character = {
			template = JAP_iemochi_tokugawa
			on_created = {
				set_character_as_ruler = yes
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			is_template_used = JAP_iemochi_tokugawa
			NOT = {
				is_template_used = JAP_yoshinobu_tokugawa
			}
			game_date >= 1837.10.28
		}
		create_character = {
			template = JAP_yoshinobu_tokugawa
			on_created = {
				set_character_as_ruler = yes
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			is_template_used = JAP_yoshinobu_tokugawa
			NOT = {
				is_template_used = JAP_iesato_tokugawa
			}
			game_date >= 1863.8.24
		}
		create_character = {
			template = JAP_iesato_tokugawa
			on_created = {
				set_character_as_ruler = yes
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			c:JAP ?= {
				any_scope_character = {
					character_is_valid_for_events = yes
					NOR = {
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
						is_heir_of_own_country = yes
					}
					has_variable = house_tokugawa
				}
			}
		}
		c:JAP ?= {
			ordered_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					character_is_valid_for_events = yes
					NOR = {
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
						is_heir_of_own_country = yes
					}
					has_variable = house_tokugawa
				}
				order_by = prominence
				transfer_character = c:EZO
				set_character_as_ruler = yes
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_character = {
			template = JAP_backup_shogun_template
		}
	}
}

country_ezo_set_up_as_colony = {
	set_country_type = colonial
	activate_law = law_type:law_colonial_administration
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_scope_state = {
				state_region = s:STATE_HOKKAIDO
				state_is_eligible_as_mass_migration_target = yes
			}
		}
		s:STATE_HOKKAIDO.region_state:EZO ?= {
			create_mass_migration = {
				origin = top_overlord
				culture = cu:japanese
			}
		}
	}
	remove_variable = ezo_waiting_for_shogun
}

country_ezo_setup = {
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = establish_ezo_republic_tt
		every_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = tokugawa_exile
			}
			transfer_character = c:EZO
		}
		s:STATE_HOKKAIDO ?= {
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					owner ?= scope:tokugawa_scope
				}
				set_state_owner = c:EZO
			}
		}
		c:EZO ?= {
			copy_laws = scope:tokugawa_scope
			activate_law = law_type:law_presidential_republic
			hidden_effect = { 
				activate_law = law_type:law_intermediate_edo_system
			}
			scope:tokugawa_exile_leader_scope ?= {
				set_character_as_ruler = yes
			}
			generate_ezo_military = yes
			hidden_effect = {
				clear_ownership_transfer_fleet = yes
			}
		}
	}
	c:EZO ?= {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_law = law_type:law_landed_voting
				}
			}
			activate_law = law_type:law_landed_voting
		}
		call_election = {
			months = 1
		}
		remove_primary_culture = cu:ainu
		set_state_religion = c:JAP.religion
		set_variable = ezo_republic_var
		set_social_hierarchy = edo_system
		capital ?= {
			STATE_HOKKAIDO_state_name_assign = yes
		}
	}
}

japan_generate_imperial_princess_effect = {
	if = { # Moriko
		limit = {
			year >= 1838
			year < 1842
		}
		create_character = {
			template = JAP_moriko_yamato
		}
	}
	if = { # Katsura
		limit = {
			year >= 1842
			year < 1862
		}
		create_character = {
			template = JAP_sumiko_yamato
		}
	}
	if = { # Chikako
		limit = {
			year >= 1862
			year < 1877
		}
		create_character = {
			template = JAP_chikako_yamato
		}
	}
	else = { # Random
		create_character = {
			last_name = "Yamato"
			female = yes
			noble = yes
			interest_group = ig_landowners
			on_created = {
				set_variable = house_yamato
				set_variable = japan_imperial_princess
			}
		}
	}
}

JAP_character_generate_new_daimyo = { # Fired on_character_death
	if = {
		limit = {
			var:daimyo_var ?= s:STATE_KYUSHU
		}
		JAP_character_generate_satsuma_daimyo = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			var:daimyo_var ?= s:STATE_KANTO
		}
		JAP_character_generate_mito_daimyo = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			var:daimyo_var ?= s:STATE_CHUGOKU
		}
		JAP_character_generate_choshu_daimyo = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			var:daimyo_var ?= s:STATE_SHIKOKU
		}
		JAP_character_generate_tokushima_daimyo = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			var:daimyo_var ?= s:STATE_KANSAI
		}
		JAP_character_generate_kishu_daimyo = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			var:daimyo_var ?= s:STATE_TOKAI
		}
		JAP_character_generate_owari_daimyo = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			var:daimyo_var ?= s:STATE_KYOTO
		}
		JAP_character_generate_hikone_daimyo = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			var:daimyo_var ?= s:STATE_HOKUSHINETSU
		}
		JAP_character_generate_kaga_daimyo = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			var:daimyo_var ?= s:STATE_TOHOKU
		}
		JAP_character_generate_sendai_daimyo = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			var:daimyo_var ?= s:STATE_HOKKAIDO
		}
		JAP_character_generate_matsumae_daimyo = yes
	}
	owner ?= {
		je_meiji_restoration_update_daimyos = yes
	}
}

	# je_meiji_restoration_update_daimyos called one step up, in japan_reconcile_revolutionary_daimyos
}

JAP_character_generate_satsuma_daimyo = { # Owner = country, ROOT = predecessor
	owner = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				s:STATE_KYUSHU ?= {
					var:daimyo_chain_id = 0
				}
				game_date >= 1809.4.28
			}
			create_character = {
				template = JAP_nariakira_shimazu
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				s:STATE_KYUSHU ?= {
					var:daimyo_chain_id = 1
				}
				game_date >= 1817.11.28
			}
			create_character = {
				template = JAP_hisamitsu_shimazu
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				s:STATE_KYUSHU ?= {
					var:daimyo_chain_id = 2
				}
				game_date >= 1840.5.22
			}
			create_character = {
				template = JAP_tadayoshi_shimazu
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				s:STATE_KYUSHU ?= {
					var:daimyo_chain_id = 3
				}
				game_date >= 1886.10.20
			}
			create_character = {
				template = JAP_tadashige_shimazu
			}
		}
		else = { # Satsuma people are typically Industrialists
			create_character = {
				last_name = Shimazu
				noble = yes
				culture = cu:japanese
				role = character_role_magnate
				role = character_role_politician
				home_region = STATE_KYUSHU
				interest_group = ig_industrialists
				on_created = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								has_global_variable = japan_daimyo_abolished
							}
						}
						set_variable = { name = daimyo_var value = s:STATE_KYUSHU }
					}
					designate_as_tozama_daimyo = yes
				}
			}
		}
		s:STATE_KYUSHU ?= {
			change_variable = { name = daimyo_chain_id add = 1 }
		}
	}
}

JAP_character_generate_mito_daimyo = { # Owner = country, ROOT = predecessor
	owner = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				s:STATE_KANTO ?= {
					var:daimyo_chain_id = 0
				}
				game_date >= 1832.5.3
			}
			create_character = {
				template = JAP_yoshiatsu_tokugawa
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				s:STATE_KANTO ?= {
					var:daimyo_chain_id = 1
				}
				game_date >= 1853.10.26
			}
			create_character = {
				template = JAP_akitake_tokugawa
			}
		}
		else = { # Mito people are typically Armed Forces
			create_character = {
				last_name = Tokugawa
				noble = yes
				culture = cu:japanese
				role = character_role_magnate
				role = character_role_politician
				home_region = STATE_KANTO
				interest_group = ig_armed_forces
				on_created = {
					set_variable = house_tokugawa
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								has_global_variable = japan_daimyo_abolished
							}
						}
						set_variable = { name = daimyo_var value = s:STATE_KANTO }
					}
					designate_as_shinpan_daimyo = yes
				}
			}
		}
		s:STATE_KANTO ?= {
			change_variable = { name = daimyo_chain_id add = 1 }
		}
	}
}

JAP_character_generate_tokushima_daimyo = { # Owner = country, ROOT = predecessor
	owner = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				s:STATE_SHIKOKU ?= {
					var:daimyo_chain_id = 0
				}
				game_date >= 1821.10.14
			}
			create_character = {
				template = JAP_hachisuka_narihiro
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				s:STATE_SHIKOKU ?= {
					var:daimyo_chain_id = 1
				}
				game_date >= 1846.9.28
			}
			create_character = {
				template = JAP_hachisuka_mochiaki
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				s:STATE_SHIKOKU ?= {
					var:daimyo_chain_id = 2
				}
				game_date >= 1871.3.8
			}
			create_character = {
				template = JAP_hachisuka_maasaki
			}
		}
		else = { # No real trend with Tokushima people
			create_character = {
				last_name = Hachisuka
				noble = yes
				culture = cu:japanese
				role = character_role_magnate
				role = character_role_politician
				home_region = STATE_SHIKOKU
				interest_group = ig_armed_forces
				on_created = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								has_global_variable = japan_daimyo_abolished
							}
						}
						set_variable = { name = daimyo_var value = s:STATE_SHIKOKU }
					}
					designate_as_tozama_daimyo = yes
				}
			}
		}
		s:STATE_SHIKOKU ?= {
			change_variable = { name = daimyo_chain_id add = 1 }
		}
	}
}

	owner = {

					var:daimyo_chain_id = 0
				}
				game_date >= 1820.6.8
			}

				template = JAP_tokugawa_narikatsu
			}
		}
		else_if = {

					var:daimyo_chain_id = 1
				}

					NOT = {
						any_scope_character = {
							has_template = JAP_iemochi_tokugawa
						}
					}
				}
				create_character = {
					template = JAP_iemochi_tokugawa
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					game_date >= 1844.3.1
				}
				create_character = {
					template = JAP_tokugawa_mochitsugu
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				s:STATE_KANSAI ?= {
					var:daimyo_chain_id = 2
				}
				game_date >= 1844.3.1
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = JAP_tokugawa_mochitsugu
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = JAP_tokugawa_mochitsugu
			}
		}
		else = { # Kishu people were typically Landowners
			create_character = {
				last_name = Tokugawa
				noble = yes
				culture = cu:japanese
				role = character_role_magnate
				role = character_role_politician
				home_region = STATE_KANSAI
				interest_group = ig_landowners
				on_created = {
					set_variable = house_tokugawa
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								has_global_variable = japan_daimyo_abolished
							}
						}
						set_variable = { name = daimyo_var value = s:STATE_KANSAI }
					}
					designate_as_shinpan_daimyo = yes
				}
			}
		}
		s:STATE_KANSAI ?= {
			change_variable = { name = daimyo_chain_id add = 1 }
		}
	}
}

JAP_character_generate_owari_daimyo = { # Owner = country, ROOT = predecessor
	owner = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				s:STATE_TOKAI ?= {
					var:daimyo_chain_id = 0
				}
				game_date >= 1810.7.14
			}
			create_character = {
				template = JAP_tokugawa_naritaka
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				s:STATE_TOKAI ?= {
					var:daimyo_chain_id = 1
				}
				game_date >= 1824.4.14
			}
			create_character = {
				template = JAP_tokugawa_yoshikatsu
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				s:STATE_TOKAI ?= {
					var:daimyo_chain_id = 2
				}
				game_date >= 1863.10.31
			}
			create_character = {
				template = JAP_tokugawa_yoshinori
			}
		}
		else = { # Owari people were typically Landowners
			create_character = {
				last_name = Tokugawa
				noble = yes
				culture = cu:japanese
				role = character_role_magnate
				role = character_role_politician
				home_region = STATE_TOKAI
				interest_group = ig_landowners
				on_created = {
					set_variable = house_tokugawa
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								has_global_variable = japan_daimyo_abolished
							}
						}
						set_variable = { name = daimyo_var value = s:STATE_TOKAI }
					}
					designate_as_shinpan_daimyo = yes
				}
			}
		}
		s:STATE_TOKAI ?= {
			change_variable = { name = daimyo_chain_id add = 1 }
		}
	}
}

JAP_character_generate_matsumae_daimyo = { # Owner = country, ROOT = predecessor
	owner = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				s:STATE_HOKKAIDO ?= {
					var:daimyo_chain_id = 0
				}
				game_date >= 1825.10.9
			}
			create_character = {
				template = JAP_matsumae_masahiro
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				s:STATE_HOKKAIDO ?= {
					var:daimyo_chain_id = 1
				}
				game_date >= 1829.12.10
			}
			create_character = {
				template = JAP_matsumae_takahiro
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				s:STATE_HOKKAIDO ?= {
					var:daimyo_chain_id = 2
				}
				game_date >= 1844.3.14
			}
			create_character = {
				template = JAP_matsumae_norihiro
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				s:STATE_HOKKAIDO ?= {
					var:daimyo_chain_id = 3
				}
				game_date >= 1865.11.2
			}
			create_character = {
				template = JAP_matsumae_nagahiro
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				s:STATE_HOKKAIDO ?= {
					var:daimyo_chain_id = 4
				}
				game_date >= 1889.5.10
			}
			create_character = {
				template = JAP_matsumae_yoshihiro_ii
			}
		}
		else = { # Were typically PB, and also cursed by illness
			create_character = {
				last_name = Matsumae
				noble = yes
				culture = cu:japanese
				role = character_role_magnate
				role = character_role_politician
				home_region = STATE_HOKKAIDO
				interest_group = ig_petty_bourgeoisie
				traits = {
					sickly
				}
				on_created = {
					random_list = {
						50 = {
							add_trait = psychological_affliction
						}
						50 = {

						}
					}
					random_list = {
						50 = {
							add_trait = tuberculosis
						}
						50 = {

						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								has_global_variable = japan_daimyo_abolished
							}
						}
						set_variable = { name = daimyo_var value = s:STATE_HOKKAIDO }
					}
					designate_as_fudai_daimyo = yes
				}
			}
		}
		s:STATE_HOKKAIDO ?= {
			change_variable = { name = daimyo_chain_id add = 1 }
		}
	}
}

country_calculate_and_cache_daimyo_loyalties_per_state = {
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = {
			has_role_of_type = magnate
			has_variable = daimyo_var
		}
		save_scope_as = temp_daimyo_scope
		var:daimyo_var ?= {
			random_scope_state ={
				limit = {
					c:JAP ?= owner
				}
				set_variable = { name = cached_daimyo_loyalty value = scope:temp_daimyo_scope.loyalty }
			}
		}
	}
}

iwakura_mission_save_learning = {
	var:expedition_leader_storage_var ?= {
		save_scope_as = iwakura_scope
	}
	var:current_expedition_location_var ?= {
		save_scope_as = current_expedition_location_scope
	}
	random_list = {
		40 = {
			var:current_expedition_location_var ?= {
				random_active_law = {
					limit = {
						save_temporary_scope_as = temp_law_scope
						type.progressiveness >= 0 # Will not learn about the glories of Traditionalism
						law_is_governance_principles_law = no # I don't love Meiji! I'm in love with... America!
						NOT = { # Japan does not have this law
							c:JAP = {
								has_law = scope:temp_law_scope.type
							}
						}
						c:JAP ?= { # Japan can enact this law
							any_law = {
								scope:temp_law_scope.type ?= this.type
								law_is_available = yes
							}
						}
					}
					save_scope_as = iwakura_law_learning
					ROOT = {
						add_to_variable_list = { name = iwakura_mission_law_learnings target = prev }
						post_notification = iwakura_mission_learns_laws
					}
				}
			}
		}
		30 = {
			trigger = {
				var:current_expedition_location_var.techs_researched > ROOT.techs_researched
			}
			change_variable = {
				name = iwakura_mission_tech_learnings
				add = {
					value = var:current_expedition_location_var.techs_researched
					subtract = ROOT.techs_researched
					divide = 2  # This can get ridiculous quickly
				}
			}
			post_notification = iwakura_mission_learns_techs
		}
		30 = {
			# Nothing ever happens
		}
	}
}

iwakura_mission_cleanup = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = iwakura_mission_returned_home
		}
		remove_variable = iwakura_mission_returned_home
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = iwakura_mission_visited_countries
		}
		remove_variable = iwakura_mission_visited_countries
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = iwakura_mission_in_continental_europe
		}
		remove_variable = iwakura_mission_in_continental_europe
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = iwakura_mission_law_learnings
		}
		remove_variable = iwakura_mission_law_learnings
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = iwakura_mission_tech_learnings
		}
		remove_variable = iwakura_mission_tech_learnings
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = iwakura_world_political_progressiveness
		}
		remove_variable = iwakura_world_political_progressiveness
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = iwakura_world_political_index
		}
		remove_variable = iwakura_world_political_index
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = recalled_iwakura_mission
		}
		remove_variable = recalled_iwakura_mission
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = iwakura_mission_en_route_home
		}
		remove_variable = iwakura_mission_en_route_home
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = expedition_leader_storage_var
		}
		remove_variable = expedition_leader_storage_var
	}
}

iwakura_calculate_world_politics_progressiveness = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable_list = iwakura_mission_law_learnings # Avoid divide-by-zero
		}
		set_variable = { name = iwakura_world_political_progressiveness value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = iwakura_world_political_index value = 0 }
		every_in_list = {
			variable = iwakura_mission_law_learnings
			ROOT = {
				change_variable = {
					name = iwakura_world_political_progressiveness
					add = prev.type.progressiveness
				}
				change_variable = {
					name = iwakura_world_political_index
					add = 1
				}
			}
		}
		change_variable = { name = iwakura_world_political_progressiveness divide = var:iwakura_world_political_index }
	}
}

iwakura_character_change_ideology_with_world_politics = {
	random_list = {
		1 = {
			set_ideology = ideology:ideology_modernizer_leader
		}
		1 = { # Land Reformer
			trigger = {
				owner = { has_variable = iwakura_world_political_progressiveness }
				owner.var:iwakura_world_political_progressiveness >= 20 # Note - Land Reformer's average progressiveness is 43.75
				ideology_land_reformer_valid_trigger = yes
				owner ?= {
					any_in_list = {
						variable = iwakura_mission_law_learnings
						OR = {
							law_type:law_peasant_proprietorship ?= this.type
							law_type:law_homesteading ?= this.type
						}
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				owner ?= {
					every_in_list = {
						variable = iwakura_mission_law_learnings
						limit = {
							OR = {
								law_type:law_peasant_proprietorship ?= this.type
								law_type:law_homesteading ?= this.type
							}
						}
						add = 1
					}
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip_no_bullet = land_reform_laws_in_foreign_countries_tt
			set_ideology = ideology:ideology_land_reformer
		}
		1 = { # Utopian
			trigger = {
				owner = { has_variable = iwakura_world_political_progressiveness }
				owner.var:iwakura_world_political_progressiveness >= 75 # Note - Utopian's average progressiveness is 101.38
				ideology_utopian_valid_trigger = yes
				owner ?= {
					any_in_list = {
						variable = iwakura_mission_law_learnings
						OR = { # Various laws Utopians like
							law_type:law_peasant_proprietorship ?= this.type
							law_type:law_homesteading ?= this.type
							law_type:law_collectivized_agriculture ?= this.type
							law_type:law_cooperative_ownership ?= this.type
							law_type:law_council_republic ?= this.type
							law_type:law_worker_protections ?= this.type
						}
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				owner ?= {
					every_in_list = {
						variable = iwakura_mission_law_learnings
						limit = {
							OR = { # Various laws Utopians like
								law_type:law_peasant_proprietorship ?= this.type
								law_type:law_homesteading ?= this.type
								law_type:law_collectivized_agriculture ?= this.type
								law_type:law_cooperative_ownership ?= this.type
								law_type:law_council_republic ?= this.type
								law_type:law_worker_protections ?= this.type
							}
						}
						add = 1
					}
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip_no_bullet = socialist_laws_in_foreign_countries_tt
			set_ideology = ideology:ideology_utopian
		}
		1 = { # Market Liberal
			trigger = {
				owner = { has_variable = iwakura_world_political_progressiveness }
				owner.var:iwakura_world_political_progressiveness >= 12.5 # Note - Market Liberal's average progressiveness is 12.5
				ideology_market_liberal_valid_trigger = yes
				owner ?= {
					any_in_list = {
						variable = iwakura_mission_law_learnings
						OR = { # Various laws Market Liberals like
							law_type:law_laissez_faire ?= this.type
							law_type:law_free_trade ?= this.type
							law_type:law_commercialized_agriculture ?= this.type
							law_type:law_combination_acts ?= this.type
						}
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				owner ?= {
					every_in_list = {
						variable = iwakura_mission_law_learnings
						limit = {
							OR = { # Various laws Market Liberals like
								law_type:law_laissez_faire ?= this.type
								law_type:law_free_trade ?= this.type
								law_type:law_commercialized_agriculture ?= this.type
								law_type:law_combination_acts ?= this.type
							}
						}
						add = 1
					}
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip_no_bullet = market_liberal_laws_in_foreign_countries_tt
			set_ideology = ideology:ideology_market_liberal
		}
		1 = { # Protectionist
			trigger = {
				owner = { has_variable = iwakura_world_political_progressiveness }
				owner.var:iwakura_world_political_progressiveness >= 10 # Note - Protectionist's average progressiveness is 37.5
				ideology_protectionist_valid_trigger = yes
				owner ?= {
					any_in_list = {
						variable = iwakura_mission_law_learnings
						OR = { # Various laws Protectionists like.type
							law_type:law_protectionism ?= this.type
							law_type:law_interventionism ?= this.type
						}
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				owner ?= {
					every_in_list = {
						variable = iwakura_mission_law_learnings
						limit = {
							OR = { # Various laws Protectionists like.type
								law_type:law_protectionism ?= this.type
								law_type:law_interventionism ?= this.type
							}
						}
						add = 1
					}
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip_no_bullet = protectionist_laws_in_foreign_countries_tt
			set_ideology = ideology:ideology_protectionist
		}
		1 = { # Liberal
			trigger = {
				owner = { has_variable = iwakura_world_political_progressiveness }
				owner.var:iwakura_world_political_progressiveness >= 25 # Note - Liberal's average progressiveness is 64.06
				ideology_liberal_leader_valid_trigger = yes
				owner ?= {

							law_type:law_universal_suffrage ?= this.type
							law_type:law_census_voting ?= this.type
							law_type:law_wealth_voting ?= this.type
							law_type:law_elected_bureaucrats ?= this.type
							law_type:law_guaranteed_liberties ?= this.type
							law_type:law_national_guard ?= this.type
							law_type:law_protected_speech ?= this.type
							law_type:law_right_of_assembly ?= this.type
						}
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				owner ?= {
					every_in_list = {
						variable = iwakura_mission_law_learnings
						limit = {
							OR = { # Various laws Liberals like
								law_type:law_universal_suffrage ?= this.type
								law_type:law_census_voting ?= this.type
								law_type:law_wealth_voting ?= this.type
								law_type:law_elected_bureaucrats ?= this.type
								law_type:law_guaranteed_liberties ?= this.type

				owner ?= {

							law_type:law_appointed_bureaucrats ?= this.type

				}

			modifier = {

								law_type:law_state_atheism ?= this.type
								law_type:law_technocracy ?= this.type

				owner.var:iwakura_world_political_progressiveness >= 50 # Note - Humanitarian's average progressiveness is 66.66
				ideology_humanitarian_valid_trigger = yes
				owner ?= {
					any_in_list = {
						variable = iwakura_mission_law_learnings
						OR = { # Various laws Humanitarians like
							law_type:law_multicultural ?= this.type
							law_type:law_guaranteed_liberties ?= this.type
							law_type:law_protected_speech ?= this.type
							law_type:law_womens_suffrage ?= this.type
							law_type:law_compulsory_primary_school ?= this.type
							law_type:law_no_migration_controls ?= this.type
						}
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				owner ?= {
					every_in_list = {
						variable = iwakura_mission_law_learnings
						limit = {
							OR = { # Various laws Humanitarians like
								law_type:law_multicultural ?= this.type
								law_type:law_guaranteed_liberties ?= this.type
								law_type:law_protected_speech ?= this.type
								law_type:law_womens_suffrage ?= this.type
								law_type:law_compulsory_primary_school ?= this.type
								law_type:law_no_migration_controls ?= this.type
							}
						}
						add = 1
					}
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip_no_bullet = humanitarian_laws_in_foreign_countries_tt
			set_ideology = ideology:ideology_humanitarian
		}
		1 = { # Social Democratic
			trigger = {
				owner = { has_variable = iwakura_world_political_progressiveness }
				owner.var:iwakura_world_political_progressiveness >= 50 # Note - SocDem's average progressiveness is 67.85
				ideology_social_democrat_valid_trigger = yes
				owner ?= {
					any_in_list = {
						variable = iwakura_mission_law_learnings
						OR = { # Various laws Social Democrats like
							law_type:law_worker_protections ?= this.type
							law_type:law_regulatory_bodies ?= this.type
							law_type:law_universal_suffrage ?= this.type
							law_type:law_graduated_taxation ?= this.type
							law_type:law_old_age_pension ?= this.type
							law_type:law_right_to_associate ?= this.type
						}
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				owner ?= {
					every_in_list = {
						variable = iwakura_mission_law_learnings
						limit = {
							OR = { # Various laws Social Democrats like
								law_type:law_worker_protections ?= this.type
								law_type:law_regulatory_bodies ?= this.type
								law_type:law_universal_suffrage ?= this.type
								law_type:law_graduated_taxation ?= this.type
								law_type:law_old_age_pension ?= this.type
								law_type:law_right_to_associate ?= this.type
							}
						}
						add = 1
					}
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip_no_bullet = social_democratic_laws_in_foreign_countries_tt
			set_ideology = ideology:ideology_social_democrat
		}
		1 = { # Fascist
			trigger = {
				owner = { has_variable = iwakura_world_political_progressiveness }
				owner.var:iwakura_world_political_progressiveness >= 4 # Note - Fascist's average progressiveness is 4
				ideology_fascist_valid_trigger = yes
				owner ?= {
					any_in_list = {
						variable = iwakura_mission_law_learnings
						OR = { # Various laws Fascists like
							law_type:law_single_party_state ?= this.type
							law_type:law_ethnostate ?= this.type
							law_type:law_mass_conscription ?= this.type
							law_type:law_outlawed_dissent ?= this.type
							law_type:law_corporate_state ?= this.type
							law_type:law_corporatized_unions ?= this.type
						}
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				owner ?= {
					every_in_list = {
						variable = iwakura_mission_law_learnings
						limit = {
							OR = { # Various laws Fascists like
								law_type:law_single_party_state ?= this.type
								law_type:law_ethnostate ?= this.type
								law_type:law_mass_conscription ?= this.type
								law_type:law_outlawed_dissent ?= this.type
								law_type:law_corporate_state ?= this.type
								law_type:law_corporatized_unions ?= this.type
							}
						}
						add = 1
					}
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip_no_bullet = fascist_laws_in_foreign_countries_tt
			set_ideology = ideology:ideology_fascist
		}
	}
}

character_get_and_save_coup_reason = {
    if = {
		limit = {
			owner ?= {
				any_political_lobby = {
					has_variable = coup_lobby
				}
			}
		}
		owner = {
			set_variable = { name = coup_reason_var value = 6 } # Case 6 - Foreign-Backed Coup
		}
	}
    else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_modifier = modifier_dynastic_coup
		}
		owner = {
			set_variable = { name = coup_reason_var value = 5 } # Case 5 - Dynastic Coup
		}
	}
    else_if = {
		limit = {
			save_temporary_scope_as = coupist_to_be
			owner ?= { # Case 1 - owner is enacting a law the character does not approve of
				save_temporary_scope_as = couped_to_be
				exists = currently_enacting_law
				OR = {
					scope:coupist_to_be ?= {
						law_enactment_stance = { # Refers to any_scope_character
							law = scope:couped_to_be.currently_enacting_law.type # Refers to owner
							value < neutral # Disapproves of the law
						}
					}
					AND = {
						scope:coupist_to_be ?= {
							law_enactment_stance = { # Refers to any_scope_character
								law = scope:couped_to_be.currently_enacting_law.type # Refers to owner
								value < approve # Character does not approve of the law
							}
							interest_group ?= {
								law_enactment_stance = { # Refers to any_scope_character's interest group
									law = scope:couped_to_be.currently_enacting_law.type # Refers to owner
									value < neutral # Interest group opposes the law
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		owner = {
			set_variable = { name = coup_reason_var value = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			this.interest_group = {
				has_modifier = modifier_failed_to_meet_petition # Case 2 - owner has failed a petition for this.interest_group
			}
		}
		owner = {
			set_variable = { name = coup_reason_var value = 2 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			this.interest_group ?= {
				has_modifier = negotiation_failed_quest # Case 3 - owner has failed a negotiation for this.interest_group
			}
		}
		owner = {
			set_variable = { name = coup_reason_var value = 3 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_trait = special_character_napoleonic_return # Case 4 - this just has a coupiful personality
		}
		owner = {
			set_variable = { name = coup_reason_var value = 4 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_trait = special_character_blanqui_is_free
		}
		owner = {
			set_variable = { name = coup_reason_var value = 7 } # Case 7 - Louis Auguste Blanqui
		}
	}
	else = {
		owner = {
			set_variable = { name = coup_reason_var value = 0 } # If 0, something is broken
		}
	}
}

designate_as_fudai_daimyo = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_global_variable = japan_daimyo_abolished
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				owner ?= {
					OR = {
						has_law = law_type:law_bakufu
						country_is_taikunate = yes
					}
				}
			}
			add_modifier = modifier_fudai_daimyo
		}
		else = {
			add_modifier = modifier_fudai_daimyo_restoration
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_role = character_role_politician
		}
		add_career_length = {
			role = character_role_politician
			years = 50
			random_range = { 1.0 1.5 } # 50-65 years
		}
	}
}

country_clear_daimyo = {
	set_global_variable = japan_daimyo_abolished # Set globally just in case
	every_character = { # To handle exiled characters, the void, etcetera - it is very important that all Daimyo are removed
		limit = {
			has_variable = daimyo_var
		}
		character_clear_daimyo_status = yes
	}
}

country_assess_daimyo_alignments = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_law = law_type:law_bakufu
				country_is_taikunate = yes
			}
		}
		every_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = daimyo_var
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_modifier = modifier_fudai_daimyo_restoration
				}
				remove_modifier = modifier_fudai_daimyo_restoration
				add_modifier = modifier_fudai_daimyo
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_modifier = modifier_tozama_daimyo_restoration
				}
				remove_modifier = modifier_tozama_daimyo_restoration
				add_modifier = modifier_tozama_daimyo
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_modifier = modifier_shinpan_daimyo_restoration
				}
				remove_modifier = modifier_shinpan_daimyo_restoration
				add_modifier = modifier_shinpan_daimyo
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		every_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = daimyo_var
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_modifier = modifier_fudai_daimyo
				}
				remove_modifier = modifier_fudai_daimyo
				add_modifier = modifier_fudai_daimyo_restoration
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_modifier = modifier_tozama_daimyo
				}
				remove_modifier = modifier_tozama_daimyo
				add_modifier = modifier_tozama_daimyo_restoration
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_modifier = modifier_shinpan_daimyo
				}
				remove_modifier = modifier_shinpan_daimyo
				add_modifier = modifier_shinpan_daimyo_restoration
			}
		}
	}
}

character_clear_daimyo_status = {
	remove_variable = daimyo_var
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_modifier = modifier_fudai_daimyo
		}
		remove_modifier = modifier_fudai_daimyo
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_modifier = modifier_tozama_daimyo
		}
		remove_modifier = modifier_tozama_daimyo
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_modifier = modifier_shinpan_daimyo
		}
		remove_modifier = modifier_shinpan_daimyo
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_modifier = modifier_fudai_daimyo_restoration
		}
		remove_modifier = modifier_fudai_daimyo_restoration
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_modifier = modifier_tozama_daimyo_restoration
		}
		remove_modifier = modifier_tozama_daimyo_restoration
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_modifier = modifier_shinpan_daimyo_restoration
		}
		remove_modifier = modifier_shinpan_daimyo_restoration
	}
}

evaluate_matsumae_curse = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					has_template = JAP_matsumae_yoshihiro
					year >= 1839
					is_immortal = no
				}
				AND = {
					has_template = JAP_matsumae_masahiro
					year >= 1849
					is_immortal = no
				}
				AND = {
					has_template = JAP_matsumae_takahiro
					year >= 1866
					is_immortal = no
				}
				AND = {
					has_template = JAP_matsumae_nagahiro
					year >= 1905
					is_immortal = no
				}
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			1 = {
				kill_character = yes
			}
			11 = {
				
			}
		}
	}
}

meiji_civil_war_effects = { # Executed from on_revolution_start
	# Root = Country
	# scope:target = Uprising country
	if = {
		limit = {
			var:emperor_var ?= {
				NOT = {
					owner ?= ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		var:emperor_var ?= { # Get back here
			transfer_character = ROOT
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable_list = daimyos_list
		}
		every_in_list = {
			limit = {
				AND = { # Daimyo on the opposing side whose states did not defect
					NOT = {
						owner ?= ROOT
					}
					var:daimyo_var ?= { # Their state region
						any_scope_state = {
							owner ?= ROOT
						}
					}
				}
			}
			variable = daimyos_list
			transfer_character = ROOT # Get back here
		}
		every_in_list = {
			limit = {
				AND = { # Daimyo on this side whose states did defect
					owner ?= ROOT
					var:daimyo_var ?= { # Their state region
						NOT = {
							any_scope_state = {
								owner ?= ROOT
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			variable = daimyos_list
			transfer_character = scope:target # Get outta here
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:target ?= {
				country_has_monarchy_law = yes
			}
		}
		scope:target ?= {
			every_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = daimyo_var
				}
				add_character_role = character_role_magnate # Restore Daimyo status manually
				if = {
					limit = {
						this.interest_group ?= {
							leader ?= {
								NOT = {
									has_variable = daimyo_var
								}
							}
						}
					}
					interest_group.leader ?= {
						remove_character_role = character_role_ig_leader
					}
					set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
					add_character_role = character_role_ig_leader
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:target ?= {
				has_law = law_type:law_monarchy
				var:civil_war_type_var ?= movement_type:movement_meiji_restorationist
			}
		}
		if = { # Sets up ruler of the restorationist coalition
			limit = {
				scope:target ?= {
					ruler ?= {
						is_historical = no # A historical non-daimyo is totally fine
						NOT = {
							has_variable = daimyo_var
						}
					}
				}
			}
			ordered_in_list = {
				limit = {
					scope:target ?= owner
				}
				variable = daimyos_list
				order_by = prominence
				position = 0
				add_character_role = character_role_ruler
			}
		}
		scope:target ?= {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_law = law_type:law_isolationism
				}
				activate_law = law_type:law_sakoku
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_law = law_type:law_bakufu
					country_has_former_shogun_in_government = yes
					country_is_taikunate = yes
					sonnojoi_have_shojoi_ideology = yes
				}
			}
			trigger_event = { id = ep2_meiji.41 popup = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOR = {
					has_law = law_type:law_bakufu
					country_is_taikunate = yes
				}
			}
			scope:target ?= {
				set_variable = sonnojoi_contrast_color # So that the Sonnojoi use a different colour than the Empire
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:target ?= {
				has_law = law_type:law_monarchy
				var:civil_war_type_var ?= movement_type:movement_tokugawa_loyalist
			}
		}
		scope:target ?= {
			activate_law = law_type:law_bakufu
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_law = law_type:law_peasant_levies
				}
				activate_law = law_type:law_warrior_caste
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_is_abolishing_bakufu = yes
			}
			currently_enacting_law = {
				ROOT = {
					activate_law = currently_enacting_law.type
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_law = law_type:law_bakufu
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_character = { # This is a sin, but necessary
						has_variable = former_shogun_var # designates Taikun
					}
				}
				random_character = {
					limit = {
						has_variable = former_shogun_var # designates Taikun
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								scope:target ?= owner
							}
						}
						transfer_character = scope:target
					}
					set_character_as_ruler = yes
				}
			}
			trigger_event = { id = ep2_meiji.4 popup = yes }
		}
	}
	# AI Ezochi always sides with the Matsumae
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = c:EZO
			c:EZO ?= { is_player = no }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				p:x40BDB8.state ?= {
					owner ?= scope:target
				}
				c:EZO ?= { top_overlord ?= ROOT }
			}
			scope:target ?= {
				transfer_subject = c:EZO
			}
			random_diplomatic_play = {
				limit = {
					is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_revolution
					initiator ?= {
						scope:target ?= this
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						initiator ?= {
							scope:target ?= this
						}
					}
					add_initiator_backers = { c:EZO }
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = ROOT
					target_country = c:EZO
					type = annex_country
					primary_demand = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				p:x40BDB8.state ?= {
					owner ?= ROOT
				}
				c:EZO ?= { top_overlord ?= scope:target }
			}
			ROOT ?= {
				transfer_subject = c:EZO
			}
			random_diplomatic_play = {
				limit = {
					is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_revolution
					initiator ?= {
						scope:target ?= this
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						initiator ?= {
							scope:target ?= this
						}
					}
					add_target_backers = { c:EZO }
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = scope:target
					target_country = c:EZO
					type = annex_country
					primary_demand = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				c:EZO ?= {
					is_subject = yes
					OR = {
						c:EZO ?= { top_overlord ?= ROOT }
						c:EZO ?= { top_overlord ?= scope:target }
					}
				}
			}
			random_diplomatic_play = {
				limit = {
					is_diplomatic_play_type = dp_revolution
					initiator ?= {
						scope:target ?= this
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						c:EZO ?= { top_overlord ?= scope:target }
					}
					add_war_goal = {
						holder = ROOT
						target_country = c:EZO
						type = annex_country
						primary_demand = yes
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						c:EZO ?= { top_overlord ?= ROOT }
					}
					add_war_goal = {
						holder = scope:target
						target_country = c:EZO
						type = annex_country
						primary_demand = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

japan_replace_missing_daimyo = {
	every_state_in_home_islands = { # Iterates through all Japanese states, and checks for those that do not have a sitting daimyo
		limit = {
			NOT = { state_region = s:STATE_RYUKYU_ISLANDS } # Has no Daimyo
			save_temporary_scope_as = daimyo_polled_state
			owner ?= {
				this ?= ROOT
				ROOT ?= {
					NOT = {
						any_scope_character = {
							var:daimyo_var ?= scope:daimyo_polled_state.state_region
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		JAP_state_generate_new_daimyo = yes
	}
}

# Root = Country
# scope:target = the country the wargoal was enforced against
# scope:diplomatic_play = the relevant diplomatic play
# scope:wargoal_impact = the impact of the wargoal enforced
japan_reconcile_revolutionary_daimyos = { # Fired from on_wargoal_enforced
	scope:target ?= {
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = var:emperor_var
			}
			var:emperor_var ?= {
				transfer_character = ROOT
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					ROOT = {
						country_has_monarchy_law = yes
						NOT = {
							has_law = law_type:law_bakufu
						}
					}
				}
				var:emperor_var ?= {
					set_character_as_ruler = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	japan_replace_missing_daimyo = yes
	je_meiji_restoration_update_daimyos = yes
}

japan_display_add_meiji_reforms = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NAND = {
				in_default = no
				any_scope_state = {
					filter = {
						is_incorporated = yes
					}
					any_scope_building = {
						is_building_type = building_urban_center
						level >= 5
					}
					has_building = building_railway
					percent > 0.7
				}
			}
		}
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_meiji_economy
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NAND = {
				NOT = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_serfdom }
				ig:ig_armed_forces ?= {
					is_powerful = no
					is_in_government = no
				}
				has_technology_researched = napoleonic_warfare
				NOT = {
					any_scope_building = {
						is_building_type = building_barrack
						OR = {
							has_active_production_method = pm_no_organization
							# has_active_production_method = pm_no_artillery
							# has_active_production_method = pm_cannon_artillery
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_meiji_army
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_dlc_feature = ep2_content
			}
			NAND = {
				NOT = { has_variable = completed_je_meiji_diplomacy }
				NOT = { is_country_type = unrecognized }
				NOT = { has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_isolationism }
			}
		}
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_meiji_diplomacy
		}
	}
}

transition_off_of_monarchy_law_effects = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			c:JAP ?= this
			any_scope_character = {
				has_trait = special_character_japanese_emperor
			}
		}
		every_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_trait = special_character_japanese_emperor
			}
			remove_trait = special_character_japanese_emperor
			add_character_role = character_role_politician
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_role = character_role_emperor_of_japan
				}
				remove_character_role = character_role_emperor_of_japan
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_journal_entry = je_meiji_restoration
				}
			}
			remove_variable = emperor_var
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_law = law_type:law_neo_absolutism
		}
		show_as_tooltip = {
			deactivate_law = law_type:law_neo_absolutism
		}
		activate_law = law_type:law_autocracy
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_law = law_type:law_bakufu
		}
		show_as_tooltip = {
			deactivate_law = law_type:law_bakufu
		}
		activate_law = law_type:law_autocracy
	}
}

transition_off_of_monarchy_effects = {
	crown_land_republic_effect = yes
	transition_off_of_monarchy_law_effects = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_interest_group = {
				has_ideology = ideology:ideology_constitutionalist
			}
		}
		every_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				has_ideology = ideology:ideology_constitutionalist
			}
			remove_ideology = ideology_constitutionalist
			add_ideology = ideology_republican
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOR = {
				has_journal_entry = je_abolish_monarchy
				has_variable = monarchy_stamped_out_var
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = abolishing_monarchy_var
			value = 0
		}
		add_journal_entry = { type = je_abolish_monarchy }
	}
}

country_switch_sonnojoi_ideology = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_political_movement = {
				is_political_movement_type = movement_meiji_restorationist
				has_core_ideology = ideology:ideology_meiji_restorationist_movement_shojoi
			}
		}
		random_political_movement = {
			limit = {
				is_political_movement_type = movement_meiji_restorationist
				has_core_ideology = ideology:ideology_meiji_restorationist_movement_shojoi
			}
			set_core_ideology = ideology_meiji_restorationist_movement_daijoi
			add_character_ideology = ideology_modernizer_leader
		}
	}
}

character_handle_possible_emperor_ig_leader = { # TODO PRCAL-44673 replace with a code fix
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_role = character_role_emperor_of_japan
			has_variable = emperor_kicked_out_of_ig_leadership # If this is true, it means there is a risk of an infinite loop. Drastic measures are necessary.
		}
		owner = { # To prevent an infinite loop from happening
			create_character = {
				interest_group = prev.interest_group
				role = character_role_politician
				on_created = {
					set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
				}
			}
		}
		remove_character_role = character_role_ig_leader
		remove_character_role = character_role_politician
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_role = character_role_emperor_of_japan
			NOT = { has_variable = emperor_kicked_out_of_ig_leadership }
		}
		set_variable = emperor_kicked_out_of_ig_leadership # Infinite loop safety variable
		remove_character_role = character_role_ig_leader
		remove_character_role = character_role_politician
	}
}

generate_ezo_military = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			c:JAP ?= {
				has_technology_researched = general_staff
			}
		}
		create_military_formation = {
			type = army
			hq_region = sr:region_northeast_asia
			name = shogunate_army

			combat_unit = {
				type = unit_type:combat_unit_type_line_infantry
				state_region = s:STATE_HOKKAIDO
				count = 3
			}

			combat_unit = {
				type = unit_type:combat_unit_type_mobile_artillery
				state_region = s:STATE_HOKKAIDO
				count = 1
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			c:JAP ?= {
				has_technology_researched = line_infantry
			}
		}
		create_military_formation = {
			type = army
			hq_region = sr:region_northeast_asia
			name = shogunate_army

			combat_unit = {
				type = unit_type:combat_unit_type_line_infantry
				state_region = s:STATE_HOKKAIDO
				count = 3
			}

			combat_unit = {
				type = unit_type:combat_unit_type_cannon_artillery
				state_region = s:STATE_HOKKAIDO
				count = 1
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_military_formation = {
			type = army
			hq_region = sr:region_northeast_asia
			name = shogunate_army

			combat_unit = {
				type = unit_type:combat_unit_type_irregular_infantry
				state_region = s:STATE_HOKKAIDO
				count = 3
			}

			combat_unit = {
				type = unit_type:combat_unit_type_cannon_artillery
				state_region = s:STATE_HOKKAIDO
				count = 1
			}
		}
	}
}

on_remove_ruler_effects = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_trait = special_character_japanese_emperor
		}
		remove_trait = special_character_japanese_emperor
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_template = JAP_ninko_yamato
		}
		owner = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_dlc_feature = ep2_content
				}
				trigger_event = { id = japan_monarchy.1 }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_template = JAP_komei_yamato
			game_date >= 1852.11.3
		}
		owner = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_dlc_feature = ep2_content
				}
				trigger_event = { id = japan_monarchy.2 }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_template = JAP_meiji_yamato
			game_date >= 1879.8.31
		}
		owner = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_dlc_feature = ep2_content
				}
				trigger_event = { id = japan_monarchy.3 }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_template = JAP_taisho_yamato
			game_date >= 1901.4.29
		}
		owner = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_dlc_feature = ep2_content
				}
				trigger_event = { id = japan_monarchy.4 }
			}
		}
	}
}

character_adopt_into_tokugawa = {
	set_last_name = Tokugawa # Adopted into Tokugawa family
	set_variable = house_tokugawa
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = daimyo_var
		}
		JAP_character_generate_new_daimyo = yes
		character_clear_daimyo_status = yes
	}
}

character_appoint_new_shogun_when_no_heir = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { exists = owner.heir }
		}
		owner ?= {
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_character = {
						is_adult = yes
						has_variable = house_tokugawa
						NOT = { has_role_of_type = ruler }
					}
				}
				ordered_scope_character = {
					limit = {
						is_adult = yes
						has_variable = house_tokugawa
						NOT = { has_role_of_type = ruler }
					}
					order_by = prominence
					position = 0
					set_character_as_ruler = yes
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_variable = daimyo_var
						}
						JAP_character_generate_new_daimyo = yes
						character_clear_daimyo_status = yes
					}
					set_variable = shogun_var
				}
			}
			else = {
				create_character = {
					template = JAP_backup_shogun_template
					on_created = {
						set_variable = shogun_var
						set_character_as_ruler = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			owner.heir ?= {
				NOT = {
					has_variable = house_tokugawa
				}
			}
		}
		owner.heir ?= {
			character_adopt_into_tokugawa = yes
			set_variable = shogun_var
		}
	}
}

character_designate_new_taikun = {
	remove_variable = former_shogun_var # designates Taikun
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_modifier = modifier_taikun
		}
		remove_modifier = modifier_taikun
	}
	save_scope_as = previous_taikun_scope
	owner ?= {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_character = {
					NOT = { scope:previous_taikun_scope ?= this }
					has_variable = house_tokugawa
					is_character_alive = yes
				}
			}
			#debug_log = "[TimeKeeper.GetCurrentDate.GetString]: New Taikun designated: selected from existing Tokugawa - [THIS.GetCountry.GetNameNoFormatting]"
			ordered_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { scope:previous_taikun_scope ?= this }
					has_variable = house_tokugawa
					is_interest_group_leader = yes
					is_character_alive = yes
				}
				alternative_limit = {
					NOT = { scope:previous_taikun_scope ?= this }
					has_variable = house_tokugawa
					is_character_alive = yes
				}
				order_by = prominence
				position = 0
				set_variable = former_shogun_var
				add_modifier = modifier_taikun
				character_replace_ig_leader_with_taikun = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			create_character = {
				template = JAP_backup_shogun_template
				on_created = {
					set_variable = former_shogun_var
					add_modifier = modifier_taikun
					set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
				}
			}
			#debug_log = "[TimeKeeper.GetCurrentDate.GetString]: New Taikun designated: generated from backup template - [THIS.GetCountry.GetNameNoFormatting]"
		}
	}
}

country_designate_new_taikun = {
	every_scope_character = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = former_shogun_var
		}
		remove_variable = former_shogun_var # designates Taikun
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_modifier = modifier_taikun
			}
			remove_modifier = modifier_taikun
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_scope_character = {
				has_variable = house_tokugawa
				is_character_alive = yes
			}
		}
		#debug_log = "[TimeKeeper.GetCurrentDate.GetString]: New Taikun designated: selected from existing Tokugawa - [THIS.GetCountry.GetNameNoFormatting]"
		ordered_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = house_tokugawa
				is_interest_group_leader = yes
				is_character_alive = yes
			}
			alternative_limit = {
				has_variable = house_tokugawa
				is_character_alive = yes
			}
			order_by = prominence
			position = 0
			set_variable = former_shogun_var
			add_modifier = modifier_taikun
			character_replace_ig_leader_with_taikun = yes
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_character = {
			template = JAP_backup_shogun_template
			on_created = {
				set_variable = former_shogun_var
				add_modifier = modifier_taikun
				set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
			}
		}
		#debug_log = "[TimeKeeper.GetCurrentDate.GetString]: New Taikun designated: generated from backup template - [THIS.GetCountry.GetNameNoFormatting]"
	}
}

character_replace_ig_leader_with_taikun = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_interest_group_leader = no
		}
		#debug_log = "[TimeKeeper.GetCurrentDate.GetString]: New Taikun designated: replacing existing interest group leader ([THIS.GetCharacter.GetInterestGroup.GetLeader.GetFullNameNoFormatting]) with new Taikun ([THIS.GetCharacter.GetFullNameNoFormatting]) - [THIS.GetCharacter.GetCountry.GetNameNoFormatting]"
		set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
	}
	else = {
		#debug_log = "[TimeKeeper.GetCurrentDate.GetString]: New Taikun designated: New Taikun ([THIS.GetCharacter.GetFullNameNoFormatting]) already [THIS.GetCharacter.GetInterestGroup.GetNameNoFormatting] Interest Group Leader - [THIS.GetCharacter.GetCountry.GetNameNoFormatting]"
	}
}

on_new_ruler_sikh_maharaja_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_variable = sikh_maharaja_enthroned
			}
		}
		owner = {
			je:je_sikh_sovereignty = {
				set_bar_progress = { value = 0 name = sikh_empire_progress_bar }
			}
		}
		set_variable = sikh_maharaja_enthroned
		owner = {
			change_variable = { name = sikh_dead_maharajas_var add = 1 }
		}
	}
}

on_new_ruler_brazil_succession_rights_effect = {
	save_temporary_scope_as = portuguese_monarch_scope
	c:BRZ ?= {
		heir ?= {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_character_alive = yes
					NOT = { c:POR.ruler ?= this  }
				}
				kill_character = { hidden = yes }
			}
		}
		set_heir = scope:portuguese_monarch_scope
	}
}

on_new_ruler_japan_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = house_yamato
		}
		save_temporary_scope_as = emperor_to_be_scope
		c:JAP ?= {
			set_variable = { name = emperor_var value = scope:emperor_to_be_scope }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_trait = special_character_japanese_emperor
				}
			}
			add_trait = special_character_japanese_emperor
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				owner ?= {
					has_law = law_type:law_bakufu # If a member of the Imperial Family somehow heads the Bakufu, end it instantly
				}
			}
			owner ?= {
				deactivate_law = law_type:law_bakufu
				activate_law = law_type:law_oligarchy
			}
		}
	}
	if = { # Sitting Emperors don't need this role
		limit = {
			has_role = character_role_emperor_of_japan
		}
		remove_character_role = character_role_emperor_of_japan
	}
	if = { # Marks the Shogun as the Shogun
		limit = {
			owner ?= {
				has_law = law_type:law_bakufu
				has_gov_regency = no
			}
		}
		set_variable = shogun_var
	}
}﻿great_game_britain_progress_10 = {

complete_great_game_effect = {
	set_global_variable = persia_conflict_started
	set_global_variable = afghanistan_conflict_started
	set_global_variable = korean_protectorate_started
	set_global_variable = central_asia_consolidation_happened
	set_global_variable = kazakhstan_revolt_happened
	set_global_variable = caucasian_war_happened
	random_list = {
		50 = {
			set_global_variable = caucasus_consolidated_var
		}
		50 = {
			set_global_variable = caucasus_failure_var
		}
	}
	random_list = {
		50 = {
			set_global_variable = kazakhstan_victory_var
		}
		50 = {
			set_global_variable = kazakhstan_defeat_var
		}
	}
	set_global_variable = khanates_russia_victory_var
	random_list = {
		50 = {
			set_global_variable = korean_protectorate_success
		}
		50 = {
			set_global_variable = korean_protectorate_failure
		}
	}
	random_list = {
		50 = {
			set_global_variable = afghanistan_russia_victory_var
		}
		50 = {
			set_global_variable = afghanistan_britain_victory_var
		}
		50 = {
			set_global_variable = afghanistan_afghan_victory_var
		}
	}
	random_list = {
		50 = {
			set_global_variable = persia_russia_victory_var
		}
		50 = {
			set_global_variable = persia_britain_victory_var
		}
		50 = {
			set_global_variable = persia_persian_victory_var
		}
	}
	set_global_variable = china_border_codified_variable
	set_global_variable = manchurian_railway_variable
	set_global_variable = trans_siberian_done
	set_global_variable = consolidated_british_india_variable
	set_global_variable = contained_russian_pacific_variable
}

update_gg_progressbar_tooltips = {
	je:je_great_game_control = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				"scripted_bar_progress(great_game_core_progress_bar)" < 0
			}
			set_bar_progress = { value = 0 name = great_game_core_progress_bar }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				"scripted_bar_progress(great_game_core_progress_bar)" > 200
			}
			set_bar_progress = { value = 200 name = great_game_core_progress_bar }
		}
		set_global_variable = { name = great_game_gbr_progress value = "scripted_bar_progress(great_game_core_progress_bar)" }
		set_global_variable = { name = great_game_rus_progress value = 200 }
		change_global_variable = { name = great_game_rus_progress subtract = global_var:great_game_gbr_progress }
	}
}

clear_great_game_variables = {
	remove_global_variable = great_game_gbr_progress
	remove_global_variable = great_game_rus_progress
	remove_global_variable = great_game_british_passive_var
	remove_global_variable = great_game_russian_passive_var
	remove_global_variable = great_game_balance_passive_var
	remove_global_variable = great_game_central_asia_gdp
}

calculate_great_game_passive_progress = {
	set_global_variable = { name = great_game_russian_passive_var value = 0 }
	set_global_variable = { name = great_game_british_passive_var value = 0 }
	set_global_variable = { name = great_game_balance_passive_var value = 0 }
	set_global_variable = { name = great_game_central_asia_gdp value = 0 }

	every_state_in_great_game_region = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				region = sr:region_central_asia
				region = sr:region_greater_persia
			}
		}
		change_global_variable = { name = great_game_central_asia_gdp add = this.gdp }
	}

	change_global_variable = { name = great_game_central_asia_gdp divide = 1000 }

	c:GBR ?= {
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					region = sr:region_central_asia
					region = sr:region_greater_persia
				}
			}
			change_global_variable = { name = great_game_british_passive_var add = this.gdp }
		}
		every_subject_or_below = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_state = {
					OR = {
						region = sr:region_central_asia
						region = sr:region_greater_persia
					}
				}
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						region = sr:region_central_asia
						region = sr:region_greater_persia
					}
				}
				change_global_variable = { name = great_game_british_passive_var add = this.gdp }
			}
		}
		power_bloc ?= {
			every_power_bloc_member = {
				limit = {
					NOR = {
						c:GBR ?= this
						is_subject_of = c:GBR
					}
					any_scope_state = {
						OR = {
							region = sr:region_central_asia
							region = sr:region_greater_persia
						}
					}
				}
				every_scope_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							region = sr:region_central_asia
							region = sr:region_greater_persia
						}
					}
					change_global_variable = { name = great_game_british_passive_var add = this.gdp }
				}
			}
		}
		change_global_variable = { name = great_game_british_passive_var divide = 1000 }
		change_global_variable = { name = great_game_british_passive_var divide = global_var:great_game_central_asia_gdp }
	}

	c:RUS ?= {
		every_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					region = sr:region_central_asia
					region = sr:region_greater_persia
				}
			}
			change_global_variable = { name = great_game_russian_passive_var add = this.gdp }
		}
		every_subject_or_below = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_state = {
					OR = {
						region = sr:region_central_asia
						region = sr:region_greater_persia
					}
				}
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						region = sr:region_central_asia
						region = sr:region_greater_persia
					}
				}
				change_global_variable = { name = great_game_russian_passive_var add = this.gdp }
			}
		}
		power_bloc ?= {
			every_power_bloc_member = {
				limit = {
					NOR = {
						c:RUS ?= this
						is_subject_of = c:RUS
					}
					any_scope_state = {
						OR = {
							region = sr:region_central_asia
							region = sr:region_greater_persia
						}
					}
				}
				every_scope_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							region = sr:region_central_asia
							region = sr:region_greater_persia
						}
					}
					change_global_variable = { name = great_game_russian_passive_var add = this.gdp }
				}
			}
		}
		change_global_variable = { name = great_game_russian_passive_var divide = 1000 }
		change_global_variable = { name = great_game_russian_passive_var divide = global_var:great_game_central_asia_gdp  }
	}

	set_global_variable = { name = great_game_balance_passive_var value = global_var:great_game_russian_passive_var }
	change_global_variable = { name = great_game_balance_passive_var subtract = global_var:great_game_british_passive_var }
	change_global_variable = { name = great_game_balance_passive_var multiply = 5 }
}

handle_ai_great_game_objectives = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			c:RUS ?= this
		}
		assign_russia_ai_great_game_objectives = yes
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			c:GBR ?= this
			has_journal_entry = je_great_game_control
		}
		assign_britain_ai_great_game_objectives = yes
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			is_afghan_nation = yes
			has_journal_entry = je_great_game_control
		}
		assign_afghanistan_ai_great_game_objectives = yes
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			c:PER ?= this
			has_journal_entry = je_great_game_control
		}
		assign_persia_ai_great_game_objectives = yes
	}
}

assign_russia_ai_great_game_objectives = {
	### Generic Objectives
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_variable = je_tibet_expedition_ai_var
			}
			has_variable = tibet_has_started
			NOT = { has_global_variable = tibet_expedition_completed }
			has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_himalayas
		}
		set_variable = je_tibet_expedition_ai_var
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_tibet_expedition
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_variable = je_consolidate_afghanistan_objective_ai_var
			}
			has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_greater_persia
			exists = c:AFG
		}
		set_variable = je_consolidate_afghanistan_objective_ai_var
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_consolidate_afghanistan_objective
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_variable = je_maintain_afghan_protectorate_ai_var
			}
			NOT = {
				has_global_variable = gg_afghanistan_protectorate_done
			}
			has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_greater_persia
		}
		set_variable = je_maintain_afghan_protectorate_ai_var
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_maintain_afghan_protectorate
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_variable = je_secure_influence_over_persia_ai_var
			}
			has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_greater_persia
		}
		set_variable = je_secure_influence_over_persia_ai_var
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_secure_influence_over_persia
		}
	}

	### Specific Objectives
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_variable = je_secure_persian_border_ai_var
			}
			exists = c:CIR
		}
		set_variable = je_secure_persian_border_ai_var
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_secure_persian_border
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_variable = je_pacify_kazakh_steppes_ai_var
			}
			has_journal_entry = je_pacify_the_steppes
		}
		set_variable = je_pacify_kazakh_steppes_ai_var
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_pacify_kazakh_steppes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_variable = je_codify_chinese_border_ai_var
			}
			has_technology_researched = nationalism
		}
		set_variable = je_codify_chinese_border_ai_var
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_codify_chinese_border
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_variable = je_consolidate_central_asia_ai_var
			}
			has_technology_researched = nationalism
		}
		set_variable = je_consolidate_central_asia_ai_var
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_consolidate_central_asia
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_variable = je_chinese_concessions_ai_var
			}
			has_technology_researched = railways
			any_scope_state = {
				OR = {
					state_region = s:STATE_OUTER_MANCHURIA
					state_region = s:STATE_AMUR
				}
				is_incorporated = yes
			}
		}
		set_variable = je_chinese_concessions_ai_var
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_chinese_concessions
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_variable = je_acquire_korean_protectorate_ai_var
			}
			any_neighbouring_state = {
				owner = c:KOR
				is_incorporated = yes
			}
			c:KOR ?= {
				is_country_type = unrecognized
			}
		}
		set_variable = je_acquire_korean_protectorate_ai_var
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_acquire_korean_protectorate
		}
	}
}

assign_britain_ai_great_game_objectives = {
	### Generic Objectives
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_variable = je_tibet_expedition_ai_var
			}
			has_variable = tibet_has_started
			NOT = { has_global_variable = tibet_expedition_completed }
			has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_himalayas
		}
		set_variable = je_tibet_expedition_ai_var
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_tibet_expedition
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_variable = je_consolidate_afghanistan_objective_ai_var
			}
			has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_greater_persia
			exists = c:AFG
		}
		set_variable = je_consolidate_afghanistan_objective_ai_var
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_consolidate_afghanistan_objective
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_variable = je_maintain_afghan_protectorate_ai_var
			}
			NOT = {
				has_global_variable = gg_afghanistan_protectorate_done
			}
			has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_greater_persia
		}
		set_variable = je_maintain_afghan_protectorate_ai_var
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_maintain_afghan_protectorate
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_variable = je_secure_influence_over_persia_ai_var
			}
			has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_greater_persia
		}
		set_variable = je_secure_influence_over_persia_ai_var
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_secure_influence_over_persia
		}
	}
	### Specific Objectives
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_variable = je_disrupt_russian_caucasus_ai_var
			}
			exists = c:CIR
		}
		set_variable = je_disrupt_russian_caucasus_ai_var
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_disrupt_russian_caucasus
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_variable = je_counter_russian_pacific_influence_ai_var
			}
			c:RUS ?= {
				any_scope_state = {
					region = sr:region_northeast_asia
					OR = {
						any_scope_building = {
							is_building_type = building_port
							level >= 5
						}
						any_scope_building = {
							is_building_type = building_naval_administration
							level >= 3
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = je_counter_russian_pacific_influence_ai_var
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_counter_russian_pacific_influence
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_variable = je_consolidate_british_india_ai_var
			}
			c:BIC ?= {
				is_subject_of = c:GBR
			}
		}
		set_variable = je_consolidate_british_india_ai_var
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_consolidate_british_india
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_variable = je_pamir_expedition_objective_ai_var
			}
			c:RUS ?= {
				OR = {
					any_scope_state = {
						state_region = s:STATE_TAJIKISTAN
					}
					any_subject_or_below = {
						any_scope_state = {
							state_region = s:STATE_TAJIKISTAN
						}
					}
				}
			}
			NOT = {
				has_variable = dismissed_pamir_expedition
			}
		}
		set_variable = je_pamir_expedition_objective_ai_var
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_pamir_expedition_objective
		}
	}
}
